4036:       }

4395: //  PRINT SCREEN
4396: 
4397: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
4398: 
4399: function PrintScreen({ showToast }: any) {
4400: 
4401:   const [code, setCode] = useState('');
4402: 
4403:   const [report, setReport] = useState<any>(null);
4404: 
4405:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
4406: 
4407:   const ref = useRef<View>(null);
4408: 
4409: 
4410: 
4411:   const loadReport = async () => {
4412: 
4413:     if (!code.trim()) return showToast('کد را وارد کن', true);
4414: 
4415:     setLoading(true);
4416: 
4417:     try {
4418: 
4419:       const c2 = code.trim();
4420: 
4421:       const [_salesRpt, _purchRpt] = await Promise.all([
4422:         getSalesRowsForReport(c2),
4423:         getPurchasesRowsForReport(c2),
4424:       ]);
4425:       const salesAsCustomer = _salesRpt.customer || [];
4426:       const purchasesAsSupplier = _purchRpt.supplier || [];
4427:       const triangRecv = _salesRpt.triang || [];
4428: 
4429: 
4430: 
4431:       const hasAny = (salesAsCustomer?.length || 0) + (purchasesAsSupplier?.length || 0) + (triangRecv?.length || 0);
4432: 
4433:       if (!hasAny) { showToast('داده‌ای برای این کد یافت نشد', true); setReport(null); return; }
4434: 
4435: 
4436: 
4437:       const name = salesAsCustomer?.[0]?.customer_name
4438: 
4439:         || purchasesAsSupplier?.[0]?.supplier_name
4440: 
4441:         || triangRecv?.[0]?.account_holder || '—';
4442: 
4443:       const phone = salesAsCustomer?.[0]?.customer_phone
4444: 
4445:         || purchasesAsSupplier?.[0]?.supplier_phone || '—';
4446: 
4447: 
4448: 
4449:       const salesRows = (salesAsCustomer || []).map((r: any) => ({
4450: 
4451:         date: r.date_reg || r.date_factor, invoice: r.invoice_number,
4452: 
4453:         modelCode: r.model_code, modelName: r.model_name,
4454: 
4455:         qty: Number(r.quantity) || 0, priceUnit: Number(r.price_unit) || 0,
4456: 
4457:         total: (Number(r.quantity) || 0) * (Number(r.price_unit) || 0),
4458: 
4459:         payment: Number(r.payment) || 0,
4460: 
4461:         bank: r.bank_name || '', holder: r.account_holder || '',
4462: 
4463:         holderCode: r.account_holder_code || '',
4464: 
4465:         depositDate: r.deposit_date || '', shipping: r.shipping || '',
4466: 
4467:         desc: r.description || '',
4468: 
4469:       }));
4470: 
4471: 
4472: 
4473:       const purchaseRows = (purchasesAsSupplier || []).map((r: any) => ({
4474: 
4475:         date: r.date_reg || r.date_factor, invoice: r.invoice_number,
4476: 
4477:         manualInv: r.manual_invoice || '',
4478: 
4479:         modelCode: r.model_code, modelName: r.model_name,
4480: 
4481:         qty: Number(r.quantity) || 0, priceUnit: Number(r.price_unit) || 0,
4482: 
4483:         total: (Number(r.quantity) || 0) * (Number(r.price_unit) || 0),
4484: 
4485:         payment: Number(r.payment) || 0,
4486: 
4487:         bank: r.bank_name || '', holder: r.account_holder || '',
4488: 
4489:         payerCode: r.payer_code || '',
4490: 
4491:         depositDate: r.deposit_date || '',
4492: 
4493:         desc: r.description || '',
4494: 
4495:       }));
4496: 
4497: 
4498: 
4499:       const triangRows = (triangRecv || []).map((r: any) => ({
4500: 
4501:         date: r.date_reg || r.date_factor, invoice: r.invoice_number,
4502: 
4503:         fromName: r.customer_name, fromCode: r.customer_code, fromPhone: r.customer_phone,
4504: 
4505:         amount: Number(r.payment) || 0,
4506: 
4507:         bank: r.bank_name || '', holder: r.account_holder || '',
4508: 
4509:         depositDate: r.deposit_date || '',
4510: 
4511:       }));
4512: 
4513: 
4514: 
4515:       const totalSales = salesRows.reduce((a: number, r: any) => a + r.total, 0);
4516: 
4517:       const paidByCustomer = salesRows.reduce((a: number, r: any) => a + r.payment, 0);
4518: 
4519:       const totalPurchases = purchaseRows.reduce((a: number, r: any) => a + r.total, 0);
4520: 
4521:       const paidToSupplier = purchaseRows.reduce((a: number, r: any) => a + r.payment, 0);
4522: 
4523:       const totalTriang = triangRows.reduce((a: number, r: any) => a + r.amount, 0);
4524: 
4525: 
4526: 
4527:       const customerOwes = totalSales - paidByCustomer;
4528: 
4529:       const weOwe = totalPurchases - paidToSupplier - totalTriang;
4530: 
4531:       const net = customerOwes - weOwe;
4532: 
4533: 
4534: 
4535:       const _serial = 'STMT-' + new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10).replace(/-/g, '') + '-' + c2;
4536:       const _tracking = 'TRK-' + c2.replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/gi, '').toUpperCase().slice(-8).padStart(8, '0');
4537:       const _curLabel = (typeof currencyLabel === 'function' ? currencyLabel() : '') || 'افغانی';
4538:       const _amountInWords = numToWords(Math.abs(net)) + ' ' + _curLabel;
4539:       setReport({
4540:         code: c2, name, phone,
4541:         salesRows, purchaseRows, triangRows,
4542:         totals: { totalSales, paidByCustomer, customerOwes, totalPurchases, paidToSupplier, weOwe, totalTriang, net },
4543:         timeString: displayDate(new Date()),
4544:         serial: _serial,
4545:         tracking: _tracking,
4546:         currency: _curLabel,
4547:         amountInWords: _amountInWords,
4548:       });
4549: 
4550:     } catch (e: any) { showToast(e.message, true); }
4551: 
4552:     finally { setLoading(false); }
4553: 
4554:   };
4555: 
4556: 
4557: 
4558:   const capture = async (mode: 'share' | 'print') => {
4559: 
4560:     try {
4561: 
4562:       const uri = await captureRef(ref, { format: 'jpg', quality: 0.95 });
4563: 
4564:       if (mode === 'print') await Print.printAsync({ uri });
4565: 
4566:       else if (await Sharing.isAvailableAsync()) await Sharing.shareAsync(uri);
4567: 
4568:     } catch (e: any) { showToast(e.message, true); }
4569: 
4570:   };
4571: 
4572: 
4573: 
4574:   const exportExcel = async () => {
4575: 
4576:     if (!report) return;
4577: 
4578:     try {
4579: 
4580:       const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
4581: 
4582:       const t = report.totals;
4583: 
4584: 
4585: 
4586:       if (report.salesRows.length) {
4587: 
4588:         const data = report.salesRows.map((r: any, i: number) => ({
4589: 
4590:           '#': i + 1, 'تاریخ': displayDateOnly(r.date), 'فاکتور': r.invoice,
4591: 
4592:           'کد مدل': r.modelCode, 'نام مدل': r.modelName,
4593: 
4594:           'تعداد': r.qty, 'قیمت واحد': r.priceUnit, 'جمع': r.total,
4595: 
4596:           'پرداخت': r.payment, 'مانده': r.total - r.payment,
4597: 
4598:           'بانک': r.bank, 'صاحب حساب': r.holder, 'کد صاحب حساب': r.holderCode,
4599: 
4600:           'تاریخ واریز': displayDateOnly(r.depositDate), 'باربری': r.shipping, 'شرح': r.desc,
4601: 
4602:         }));
4603: 
4604:         data.push({ 'نام مدل': 'جمع فروش', 'جمع': totals.totalSales, 'پرداخت': totals.paidByCustomer, 'مانده': totals.customerOwes } as any);
4605: 
4606:         XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(data), 'فروش');
4607: 
4608:       }
4609: 
4610: 
4611: 
4612:       if (report.purchaseRows.length) {
4613: 
4614:         const data = report.purchaseRows.map((r: any, i: number) => ({
4615: 
4616:           '#': i + 1, 'تاریخ': displayDateOnly(r.date), 'فاکتور': r.invoice, 'فاکتور دستی': r.manualInv,
4617: 
4618:           'کد مدل': r.modelCode, 'نام مدل': r.modelName,
4619: 
4620:           'تعداد': r.qty, 'قیمت واحد': r.priceUnit, 'جمع': r.total,
4621: 
4622:           'پرداخت': r.payment, 'مانده': r.total - r.payment,
4623:

5228: 
5229: function ProfitScreen({ showToast, settings }: any) {
5230:   const profitRef = useRef<View>(null);
5231: 
5232:   const [models, setModels] = useState<any[]>([]);
5233: 
5234:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
5235: 
5236:   const [stats, setStats] = useState<any>(null);
5237: 
5238: 
5239: 
5240:   const load = async () => {
5241: 
5242:     setLoading(true);
5243: 
5244:     try { const d = await getDashboardStats(settings?.dashboard_range || 'month', settings?.dashboard_filter || 'both'); setModels(d.models); setStats(d); }
5245: 
5246:     catch (e: any) { showToast(e.message, true); } finally { setLoading(false); }
5247: 
5248:   };
5249: 
5250:   useEffect(() => { load(); }, [settings]);
5251: 
5252: 
5253: 
5254:   const total = models.reduce((a, m) => a + (m.totalProfit || 0), 0);
5255: 
5256:   const exportProfitPdf = async () => {
5257:     if (!models.length) return showToast('داده‌ای برای خروجی نیست', true);
5258:     try {
5259:       var _p = [];
5260:       var curLabel = (typeof getCurrencySync === 'function' ? (getCurrencySync().label || '') : 'افغانی');
5261:       var _serial = 'PRF-' + new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10).replace(/-/g, '') + '-' + (settings?.dashboard_range || 'month');
5262:       var _tracking = 'TRK-PRF-' + String(Date.now()).slice(-6);
5263: 
5264:       _p.push('<div class="info"><b>🆔 شماره سند:</b> ' + _serial + ' &nbsp;|&nbsp; <b>🔑 کد رهگیری:</b> ' + _tracking + '<br><b>📅 تاریخ گزارش:</b> ' + displayDate(new Date()) + ' &nbsp;|&nbsp; <b>📊 بازه:</b> ' + (stats?.rangeLabel || 'ماه جاری') + '</div>');
5265: 
5266:       _p.push('<div class="title">💹 گزارش سود به تفکیک مدل</div>');
5267:       _p.push('<table><tr><th>#</th><th>کد مدل</th><th>نام مدل</th><th>خرید (میانگین)</th><th>فروش (میانگین)</th><th>سود هر واحد</th><th>تعداد فروش</th><th>جمع سود کل</th></tr>');
5268: 
5269:       var rows = models.map(function(m, i) {
5270:         var avgSale = m.totalQty ? (m.totalSales / m.totalQty) : 0;
5271:         var unitProfit = m.purchasePrice > 0 ? (avgSale - m.purchasePrice) : 0;
5272:         return '<tr>' +
5273:           '<td>' + (i + 1) + '</td>' +
5274:           '<td>' + (m.code || '—') + '</td>' +
5275:           '<td style="text-align:right">' + (m.name || '—') + '</td>' +
5276:           '<td>' + (m.purchasePrice > 0 ? fmt(Math.round(m.purchasePrice)) : '—') + '</td>' +
5277:           '<td>' + (avgSale ? fmt(Math.round(avgSale)) : '—') + '</td>' +
5278:           '<td style="color:' + (unitProfit > 0 ? '#059669' : '#dc2626') + ';font-weight:bold">' + (unitProfit ? fmt(Math.round(unitProfit)) : '—') + '</td>' +
5279:           '<td>' + fmt(m.totalQty) + '</td>' +
5280:           '<td style="color:' + (m.totalProfit >= 0 ? '#059669' : '#dc2626') + ';font-weight:bold">' + fmt(m.totalProfit) + '</td>' +
5281:           '</tr>';
5282:       }).join('');
5283: 
5284:       _p.push(rows);

6459:

6461: 
6462:                           </View>
6463: 
6464:                         ))}
6465: 
6466:                       </View>
6467: 
6468:                     </ScrollView>
6469: 
6470:                   </View>
6471: 
6472:                 )}
6473: 
6474:                 {(modelKardexData.purchases || []).length === 0 && (modelKardexData.sales || []).length === 0 && (

6490:     </>);

6509:

6511:

6513:

6515:

6517:

6519:

6521:

6645: 
6646: function UnpaidSection({ showToast }: any) {
6647: 
6648:   const [list, setList] = useState<any[]>([]);
6649: 
6650:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
6651: 
6652:   const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
6653: 
6654: 
6655: 
6656:   const load = async () => { setLoading(true); try { setList(await getUnpaidInvoices()); } catch (e: any) { showToast(e.message, true); } finally { setLoading(false); } };
6657: 
6658:   useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);
6659: 
6660:   const toggle = (inv: string) => { const n = new Set(selected); n.has(inv) ? n.delete(inv) : n.add(inv); setSelected(n); };
6661: 
6662:   const toggleAll = () => {
6663: 
6664:     if (selected.size === list.length) setSelected(new Set());
6665: 
6666:     else setSelected(new Set(list.map((x: any) => x.invoice)));
6667: 
6668:   };
6669: 
6670:   const del = async () => {
6671: 
6672:     if (!selected.size) return;
6673: 
6674:     const ok = await confirmMsg('حذف', `${selected.size} فاکتور حذف شود؟`);
6675: 
6676:     if (!ok) return;
6677: 
6678:     try { for (const inv of Array.from(selected)) await deleteSale(inv); showToast('✅ حذف شد'); setSelected(new Set()); load(); }
6679: 
6680:     catch (e: any) { showToast(e.message, true); }
6681: 
6682:   };
6683: 
6684: 
6685: 
6686:   return (
6687: 
6688:     <View>
6689: 
6690:       <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, marginBottom: 12 }}>
6691: 
6692:         <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#334155' }]} onPress={load}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>{t('🔄 بروزرسانی')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
6693: 
6694:         <TouchableOpacity accessibilityLabel="حذف" style={[s.btn, { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#dc2626' }]} onPress={del} disabled={!selected.size}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>🗑️ حذف ({toFaNum(selected.size)})</Text></TouchableOpacity>
6695: 
6696:       </View>
6697: 
6698:       <EL text="📄 پرداخت‌نشده ({toFaNum(list.length)})" style={s.secT} />
6699: 
6700: 
6701: 
6702:       {/* جدول ۸ ستونه */}
6703: 
6704:       <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator>
6705: 
6706:         <View>
6707: 
6708:           <View style={[s.tblHeader, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse' }]}>
6709: 
6710:             <TouchableOpacity onPress={toggleAll} style={{ width: 50, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
6711: 
6712:               <Text style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>{selected.size === list.length && list.length > 0 ? '✅' : '⬜'}</Text>
6713: 
6714:             </TouchableOpacity>
6715: 
6716:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 40, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>#</Text>
6717: 
6718:             <EL textKey="فاکتور" style={[s.thCell, { width: 150 }]} />
6719: 
6720:             <EL textKey="نام" style={[s.thCell, { width: 160 }]} />
6721: 
6722:             <EL textKey="تلفن" style={[s.thCell, { width: 120 }]} />
6723: 
6724:             <EL textKey="مبلغ کل" style={[s.thCell, { width: 130 }]} />
6725: 
6726:             <EL textKey="پرداخت" style={[s.thCell, { width: 130 }]} />
6727: 
6728:             <EL textKey="وضعیت" style={[s.thCell, { width: 120 }]} />
6729: 
6730:           </View>
6731: 
6732:           {loading ? <ActivityIndicator color="#d4af37" style={{ marginTop: 20 }} /> : list.map((inv: any, i: number) => (
6733: 
6734:             <TouchableOpacity key={inv.invoice} onPress={() => toggle(inv.invoice)} style={[s.tblRow, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse' }, i % 2 === 0 && { backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }, selected.has(inv.invoice) && { backgroundColor: '#fee2e2' }]}>
6735: 
6736:               <View style={{ width: 50, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
6737: 
6738:                 <Text style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>{selected.has(inv.invoice) ? '✅' : '⬜'}</Text>
6739: 
6740:               </View>
6741: 
6742:               <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 40, fontSize: 12, color: '#d4af37', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{i + 1}</Text>
6743: 
6744:               <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 150, fontSize: 12, color: '#1e3a5f', fontWeight: 'bold', fontSize: 12 }]}>{inv.invoice}</Text>
6745: 
6746:               <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 160, fontSize: 12, color: C.text, textAlign: 'right' }]} numberOfLines={1}>{inv.name || '—'}</Text>
6747: 
6748:               <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 120, fontSize: 12, color: C.textMut, fontSize: 11 }]}>{inv.phone || '—'}</Text>
6749:

6751: 
6752:               <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 130, fontSize: 12, color: '#dc2626', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{fmt(inv.paid)}</Text>
6753: 
6754:               <View style={[s.tdCell, { width: 120, alignItems: 'center' }]}>
6755: 
6756:                 <EL textKey="❌ پرداخت‌نشده" style={{ backgroundColor: '#f8d7da', color: '#721c24', paddingHorizontal: 8, paddingVertical: 3, borderRadius: 10, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 'bold' }} />
6757: 
6758:               </View>
6759: 
6760:             </TouchableOpacity>
6761: 
6762:           ))}
6763: 
6764:         </View>
6765:

6780: //  NEW ORDER SECTION — کارت فرم سریع
6781: 
6782: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
6783: 
6784: function NewOrderSection({ showToast }: any) {
6785: 
6786:   const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
6787: 
6788:   const [name, setName] = useState('');
6789: 
6790:   const [address, setAddress] = useState('');
6791: 
6792:   const [shipping, setShipping] = useState('');
6793: 
6794:   const [invoice, setInvoice] = useState('');
6795: 
6796:   const [status, setStatus] = useState('');
6797: 
6798:   const [items, setItems] = useState<any[]>([]);
6799: 
6800:   const [prods, setProds] = useState<Product[]>([]);
6801: 
6802:   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
6803: 
6804: 
6805: 
6806:   useEffect(() => {
6807: 
6808:     getProducts().then(setProds);
6809: 
6810:     generateInvoiceNumber().then(setInvoice);
6811: 
6812:     setItems([{ modelCode: '', modelName: '', quantity: 0, priceUnit: 0, payment: 0, description: '', depositDate: toStorageDateFull(new Date()), bankName: '', accountHolder: '', accountHolderCode: '' }]);
6813: 
6814:   }, []);
6815: 
6816: 
6817: 
6818:   const onPhone = async (v: string) => {
6819: 
6820:     const d = v.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 11);
6821: 
6822:     setPhone(d); setStatus('');
6823: 
6824:     if (d.length === 11) {
6825: 
6826:       setStatus('⏳');
6827: 
6828:       try { const f = await lookupCustomerByPhone(d); if (f) { setStatus('✅'); if (f.customer_name && !name) setName(f.customer_name); if (f.customer_address && !address) setAddress(f.customer_address); } else setStatus('🆕'); } catch {}
6829: 
6830:     }
6831: 
6832:   };
6833: 
6834:   const addRow = () => setItems([...items, { modelCode: '', modelName: '', quantity: 0, priceUnit: 0, payment: 0, description: '', depositDate: toStorageDateFull(new Date()), bankName: '', accountHolder: '', accountHolderCode: '' }]);
6835: 
6836:   const upd = (i: number, f: string, v: any) => { const n = [...items]; (n[i] as any)[f] = v; setItems(n); };
6837: 
6838:   const del = (i: number) => setItems(items.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i));
6839: 
6840:   const setModel = (i: number, v: string) => { const n = [...items]; n[i].modelName = v; const p = prods.find((x) => x.name === v); if (p) { n[i].modelCode = p.code || ''; n[i].priceUnit = p.price || 0; } setItems(n); };
6841: 
6842: 
6843: 
6844:   const grandTotal = items.reduce((a, r) => a + (Number(r.quantity) || 0) * (Number(r.priceUnit) || 0), 0);
6845: 
6846:   const grandQty = items.reduce((a, r) => a + (Number(r.quantity) || 0), 0);
6847: 
6848: 
6849: 
6850:   const submit = async () => {
6851: 
6852:     if (!/^09\d{9}$/.test(phone)) return alertMsg('⚠️ خطا', 'شماره معتبر نیست');
6853: 
6854:     if (!name) return alertMsg('⚠️ خطا', 'نام الزامی است');
6855: 
6856:     if (!shipping) return alertMsg('⚠️ خطا', 'باربری الزامی است');
6857: 
6858:     const valid = items.filter((it) => it.modelName && it.quantity > 0);
6859: 
6860:     if (!valid.length) return alertMsg('⚠️ خطا', 'حداقل یک مدل');
6861: 
6862:     const ok = await confirmMsg('📋 تأیید', `👤 ${name}\n📞 ${phone}\n📦 ${valid.length} مدل\n💰 ${fmt(grandTotal)}\n\nثبت شود؟`);
6863: 
6864:     if (!ok) return;
6865: 
6866:     setSaving(true);
6867: 
6868:     try {
6869: 
6870:       let code = '';
6871: 
6872:       const f = await lookupCustomerByPhone(phone);
6873: 
6874:       if (f?.customer_code && /^M_\d+$/.test(f.customer_code)) code = f.customer_code;
6875: 
6876:       else code = await generateCustomerCode();
6877: 
6878:       const inv = await generateInvoiceNumber();
6879: 
6880:       await createSale({ invoiceNumber: inv, customerCode: code, customerName: name, customerPhone: phone, customerAddress: address, shipping, items: valid, receiverName, receiverCode, receiverBank, receiverAccount });
6881: 
6882:       alertMsg('✅ موفق', 'ثبت شد: ' + inv);
6883: 
6884:       setPhone(''); setName(''); setAddress(''); setShipping('');
6885: 
6886:       setItems([{ modelCode: '', modelName: '', quantity: 0, priceUnit: 0, payment: 0, description: '', depositDate: toStorageDateFull(new Date()), bankName: '', accountHolder: '', accountHolderCode: '' }]);
6887: 
6888:       setInvoice(await generateInvoiceNumber());
6889: 
6890:     } catch (e: any) { alertMsg('❌ خطا', e?.message || String(e)); } finally { setSaving(false); }
6891: 
6892:   };
6893: 
6894: 
6895: 
6896:   return (
6897: 
6898:     <View>
6899: 
6900:       <EL text="{t('➕ سفارش جدید')}" style={s.formTitle} />
6901: 
6902:       <EL text="📞 تلفن {status}" style={[s.lbl, { color: C.textMut }]} />
6903: 
6904:       <TextInput accessibilityLabel="09121234567" style={[s.inp, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={phone} onChangeText={onPhone} keyboardType="phone-pad" maxLength={11} placeholder="09121234567" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
6905: 
6906:       <EL text="{t('👤 نام')}" style={[s.lbl, { color: C.textMut }]} />
6907: 
6908:       <TextInput style={[s.inp, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={name} onChangeText={setName} placeholder={term('customer')} placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
6909: 
6910:       <EL text="{t('📍 آدرس')}" style={[s.lbl, { color: C.textMut }]} />
6911: 
6912:       <TextInput accessibilityLabel="اختیاری" style={[s.inp, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={address} onChangeText={setAddress} placeholder="اختیاری" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
6913: 
6914:       <Autocomplete label="🚚 باربری" value={shipping} onChange={setShipping} options={SHIPPINGS} placeholder="تایپ..." />
6915: 
6916:       <EL text="🧾 شماره فاکتور (خودکار)" style={[s.lbl, { color: C.textMut }]} />
6917: 
6918:       <TextInput style={[s.inp, { backgroundColor: C.cardAlt, color: C.textMut, borderColor: C.border }]} value={invoice} editable={false} />
6919: 
6920: 
6921: 
6922:       <EL text="{t('📦 اقلام')}" style={s.secT} />
6923: 
6924: 
6925: 
6926:       {items.map((it, i) => {
6927: 
6928:         const lt = (Number(it.quantity) || 0) * (Number(it.priceUnit) || 0);
6929: 
6930:         const lb = lt - (Number(it.payment) || 0);
6931: 
6932:         return (
6933: 
6934:           <View key={i} style={[s.itemCard, { backgroundColor: C.card, borderColor: C.border }]}>
6935: 
6936:             <View style={[s.itemHeader, { backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }]}>
6937: 
6938:               <Text style={s.itemN}>#{i + 1}</Text>
6939: 
6940:               <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => del(i)} style={s.delBtnRound}><Text style={{ fontSize: 13, color: '#fff', fontWeight: 'bold' }}>🗑</Text></TouchableOpacity>
6941: 
6942:             </View>
6943: 
6944:             <View style={{ padding: 10 }}>
6945: 
6946:               <Autocomplete value={it.modelName} onChange={(v) => upd(i, 'modelName', v)} onSelect={(v) => setModel(i, v)} options={prods.map(p => p.name)} placeholder={term('product') + '...'} />
6947: 
6948:               <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 6 }}>
6949: 
6950:                 <View style={{ flex: 1 }}><EL textKey="کد" style={[s.miniLbl, { color: C.textMut }]} /><View style={[s.miniValueBox, { backgroundColor: C.cardAlt, borderColor: C.border }]}><Text style={[s.miniValueTxt, { color: C.text }]}>{it.modelCode || '—'}</Text></View></View>
6951: 
6952:                 <View style={{ flex: 1 }}><EL textKey="تعداد" style={[s.miniLbl, { color: C.textMut }]} /><TextInput style={[s.miniInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(it.quantity || '')} onChangeText={(v) => upd(i, 'quantity', parseInt(v) || 0)} keyboardType="numeric" /></View>
6953: 
6954:                 <View style={{ flex: 1 }}><EL textKey="قیمت" style={[s.miniLbl, { color: C.textMut }]} /><TextInput style={[s.miniInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(it.priceUnit || '')} onChangeText={(v) => upd(i, 'priceUnit', parseNum(v))} keyboardType="numeric" /></View>
6955: 
6956:               </View>
6957: 
6958:               <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 6, marginTop: 6 }}>
6959: 
6960:                 <View style={{ flex: 1 }}><EL textKey="💰 مبلغ" style={[s.miniLbl, { color: C.textMut }]} /><View style={[s.miniValueBox, { backgroundColor: C.cardAlt, borderColor: lt ? '#00ff88' : C.border }]}><Text style={[s.miniValueTxt, { color: lt ? '#00ff88' : C.textMut }]}>{lt ? fmt(lt) : '—'}</Text></View></View>
6961: 
6962:                 <View style={{ flex: 1 }}><EL textKey="پرداخت" style={[s.miniLbl, { color: C.textMut }]} /><TextInput style={[s.miniInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(it.payment || '')} onChangeText={(v) => upd(i, 'payment', parseNum(v))} keyboardType="numeric" /></View>
6963: 
6964:                 <View style={{ flex: 1 }}><EL textKey="⚖️ مانده" style={[s.miniLbl, { color: C.textMut }]} /><View style={[s.miniValueBox, { backgroundColor: C.cardAlt, borderColor: lb > 0 ? '#dc2626' : '#059669' }]}><Text style={[s.miniValueTxt, { color: lb > 0 ? '#dc2626' : '#059669' }]}>{lt ? fmt(lb) : '—'}</Text></View></View>
6965: 
6966:               </View>
6967: 
6968:             </View>
6969: 
6970:           </View>
6971: 
6972:         );
6973: 
6974:       })}
6975: 
6976: 
6977: 
6978:       <TouchableOpacity style={s.addBtn2} onPress={addRow}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>{t('➕ افزودن ردیف')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
6979: 
6980:       <View style={s.grandSummary}>
6981: 
6982:         <View style={{ flex: 1 }}><EL textKey="💰 جمع کل" style={s.gsLbl} /><Text style={s.gsVal}>{fmt(grandTotal)}</Text></View>
6983: 
6984:         <View style={{ alignItems: 'center' }}><EL textKey="📦 تعداد" style={s.gsLbl} /><Text style={s.gsQty}>{toFaNum(grandQty)}</Text></View>
6985: 
6986:       </View>
6987: 
6988:       <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { backgroundColor: '#059669', marginTop: 12 }]} onPress={submit} disabled={saving}>
6989: 
6990:         {saving ? <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" /> : <Text style={s.btnTxt}>{t('✅ ثبت سفارش')}</Text>}
6991: 
6992:       </TouchableOpacity>
6993: 
6994:     </View>
6995: 
6996:   );
6997: 
6998: }
6999: 
7000: 
7001: 
7002: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
7003: 
7004: //  SEARCH SECTION — جدول ۱۲ ستونه (مثل وب)
7005: 
7006: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
7007: 
7008: function SearchSection({ showToast }: any) {
7009: 
7010:   const [list, setList] = useState<any[]>([]);
7011: 
7012:   const [prods, setProds] = useState<Product[]>([]);
7013: 
7014:   const [q, setQ] = useState('');
7015: 
7016:   const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all');
7017: 
7018:   const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('all');
7019: 
7020:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
7021: 
7022:   const [editing, setEditing] = useState<any>(null);
7023: 
7024:   const [editRows, setEditRows] = useState<any[]>([]);
7025: 
7026:   const [dirty, setDirty] = useState(false);
7027:   const [showInvoice, setShowInvoice] = useState<any>(null);
7028:   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
7029:   const savingRef = useRef(false);
7030:   const [banksList, setBanksList] = useState<any[]>([]);
7031:   const [shippingsList, setShippingsList] = useState<any[]>([]);
7032:   const [partiesList, setPartiesList] = useState<any[]>([]);
7033:   const [partnersList, setPartnersList] = useState<any[]>([]);
7034:   useEffect(() => {
7035:     getBanks().then(setBanksList).catch(()=>{});
7036:     getShippings().then(setShippingsList).catch(()=>{});
7037:     getParties().then(setPartiesList).catch(()=>{});
7038:     getPartners().then(setPartnersList).catch(()=>{});
7039:   }, []);
7040: 
7041:   const onHolderPick = async (i: number, v: string) => {
7042:     updRow(i, 'accountHolder', v);
7043:     try {
7044:       const f = await lookupPayerUniversal(v);
7045:       if (f) {
7046:         updRow(i, 'accountHolderCode', f.code || '');
7047:         if (f.bank) updRow(i, 'bankName', f.bank);
7048:       }
7049:     } catch {}
7050:   };
7051: 
7052: 
7053: 
7054:   const load = async () => { setLoading(true); try { setList(await searchAllInvoices('')); } catch (e: any) { showToast(e.message, true); } finally { setLoading(false); } };
7055: 
7056:   const loadProds = async () => { try { setProds(await getProducts()); } catch {} };
7057: 
7058:   useEffect(() => { load(); loadProds(); }, []);
7059: 
7060: 
7061: 
7062:   const filtered = list.filter((r) => {
7063: 
7064:     if (filter !== 'all' && r.type !== filter) return false;
7065: 
7066:     const balance = (r.total || 0) - (r.paid || 0);
7067: 
7068:     let st = 'unpaid';
7069: 
7070:     if (r.paid >= r.total && r.total > 0) st = 'paid';
7071: 
7072:     else if (r.paid > 0) st = 'partial';
7073: 
7074:     if (statusFilter !== 'all' && st !== statusFilter) return false;
7075: 
7076:     if (q) { const h = `${r.invoice} ${r.name || ''} ${r.phone || ''}`.toLowerCase(); if (!h.includes(q.toLowerCase())) return false; }
7077: 
7078:     return true;
7079: 
7080:   });
7081: 
7082: 
7083: 
7084:   const openEdit = async (inv: string, type: string) => {
7085: 
7086:     try {
7087: 
7088:       const rows = await getInvoiceDetail(inv, type === 'sales' ? 'sales' : 'purchase');
7089: 
7090:       if (!rows.length) { showToast('فاکتور پیدا نشد', true); return; }
7091: 
7092:       const isSales = type === 'sales';
7093: 
7094:       setEditRows(rows.map((r: any) => ({
7095: 
7096:         rowId: r.id,
7097: 
7098:         modelCode: r.model_code || '',
7099: 
7100:         modelName: r.model_name || '',
7101: 
7102:         quantity: r.quantity || 0,
7103: 
7104:         priceUnit: r.price_unit || 0,
7105: 
7106:         payment: r.payment || 0,
7107: 
7108:         description: r.description || '',
7109: 
7110:         depositDate: r.deposit_date || '',
7111: 
7112:         bankName: r.bank_name || r.receiver_bank || '',
7113:         accountHolder: r.account_holder || r.receiver_name || '',
7114:         accountHolderCode: isSales ? (r.account_holder_code || r.receiver_code || '') : (r.payer_code || ''),
7115: 
7116:         shipping: isSales ? (r.shipping || '') : '',
7117: 
7118:         _deleted: false,
7119: 
7120:       })));
7121: 
7122:       setEditing({
7123:         invoice: inv,
7124:         type,
7125:         name: isSales ? rows[0].customer_name : rows[0].supplier_name,
7126:         code: isSales ? rows[0].customer_code : rows[0].supplier_code,
7127:         phone: isSales ? (rows[0].customer_phone || '') : (rows[0].supplier_phone || ''),
7128:         manualInv: isSales ? '' : (rows[0].manual_invoice || ''),
7129:       });
7130: 
7131:       setDirty(false);
7132: 
7133:     } catch (e: any) { showToast(e.message || 'خطا در باز کردن فاکتور', true); }
7134: 
7135:   };
7136: 
7137: 
7138: 
7139:   const delRow = (i: number) => { const n = [...editRows]; n[i]._deleted = !n[i]._deleted; setEditRows(n); setDirty(true); };
7140: 
7141:   const addRow = () => { setEditRows([...editRows, { rowId: null, modelCode: '', modelName: '', quantity: 0, priceUnit: 0, payment: 0, description: '', depositDate: toStorageDateFull(new Date()), bankName: '', accountHolder: '', accountHolderCode: '', shipping: '', _deleted: false, _new: true }]); setDirty(true); };
7142: 
7143:   const updRow = (i: number, f: string, v: any) => { const n = [...editRows]; (n[i] as any)[f] = v; setEditRows(n); setDirty(true); };
7144: 
7145: 
7146: 
7147:   const previewInvoice = async () => {
7148:     const validRows = editRows.filter((r: any) => !r._deleted);
7149:     if (!validRows.length) { showToast('هیچ ردیفی برای نمایش نیست', true); return; }
7150:     const items = validRows.map((r: any) => ({
7151:       modelName: r.modelName || '',
7152:       modelCode: r.modelCode || '',
7153:       quantity: Number(r.quantity) || 0,
7154:       priceUnit: Number(r.priceUnit) || 0,
7155:       total: (Number(r.quantity) || 0) * (Number(r.priceUnit) || 0),
7156:     }));
7157:     const sales = items.reduce((a: number, it: any) => a + it.total, 0);
7158:     const payments = validRows.reduce((a: number, r: any) => a + (Number(r.payment) || 0), 0);
7159:     let prev: any = { sales: 0, payments: 0, balance: 0, invoiceCount: 0 };
7160:     try { prev = await getCustomerPreviousBalance(editing.code, editing.invoice); } catch {}
7161:     setShowInvoice({
7162:       customer: {
7163:         code: editing.code,
7164:         name: editing.name,
7165:         phone: editing.phone || '',
7166:         address: '',
7167:         shipping: validRows[0]?.shipping || '',
7168:       },
7169:       current: {
7170:         invoiceNumber: editing.invoice,
7171:         items,
7172:         sales,
7173:         payments,
7174:         balance: sales - payments,
7175:       },
7176:       previous: prev,
7177:       totals: {
7178:         sales: (prev.sales || 0) + sales,
7179:         payments: (prev.payments || 0) + payments,
7180:         balance: (prev.balance || 0) + (sales - payments),
7181:       },
7182:       timeString: displayDate(new Date().toISOString()),
7183:     });
7184:   };
7185: 
7186:   const save = async () => {
7187: 
7188:     if (!dirty) return showToast('تغییری نیست', true);
7189: 
7190:     if (savingRef.current) return;
7191:     savingRef.current = true;
7192:     setSaving(true);
7193:     try {
7194: 
7195:       const tbl = editing.type === 'sales' ? 'sales' : 'purchases';
7196: 
7197:       
7198: 
7199:       // چک کن اگه فاکتور توی صف آفلاین هست
7200: 
7201:       const anyPending = editRows.some((r: any) => r.rowId && String(r.rowId).startsWith('queued_'));
7202: 
7203:       
7204: 
7205:       if (anyPending) {
7206: 
7207:         const ok = await updateInvoiceInQueue(editing.invoice, editing.type, editRows);
7208: 
7209:         if (!ok) throw new Error('فاکتور آفلاین پیدا نشد');
7210: 
7211:         alertMsg('✅ موفق', 'تغییرات روی فاکتور آفلاین ذخیره شد');
7212: 
7213:         setEditing(null); setEditRows([]); setDirty(false); load();
7214: 
7215:         return;
7216: 
7217:       }
7218: 
7219:       // ═══ ذخیره فاکتور سینک‌شده (آفلاین یا آنلاین) ═══
7220:       const validRows = editRows.filter((r: any) => !r._deleted);
7221:       const newTotal = validRows.reduce((a: number, r: any) => a + (Number(r.quantity) || 0) * (Number(r.priceUnit) || 0), 0);
7222:       const newPaid = validRows.reduce((a: number, r: any) => a + (Number(r.payment) || 0), 0);
7223:       const meta: any = editing.type === 'sales'
7224:         ? { name: editing.name, code: editing.code, phone: editing.phone, shipping: editRows[0]?.shipping || '' }
7225:         : { name: editing.name, code: editing.code, phone: editing.phone, manualInv: editing.manualInv, payerName: editRows[0]?.accountHolder || '', payerCode: editRows[0]?.accountHolderCode || '' };
7226: 
7227:       if (editing.type === 'sales') {
7228:         await updateSale({ invoiceNumber: editing.invoice, rows: editRows, meta });
7229:       } else {
7230:         await updatePurchase({ invoiceNumber: editing.invoice, rows: editRows, meta });
7231:       }
7232: 
7233:       // آپدیت فوری لیست محلی
7234:       setList((prev: any[]) => prev.map((x: any) => x.invoice === editing.invoice
7235:         ? { ...x, total: newTotal, paid: newPaid, name: editing.name, phone: editing.phone }
7236:         : x));
7237: 
7238:       
7239: 
7240:       
7241: 
7242:       alertMsg('✅ موفق', 'تغییرات ذخیره شد');
7243: 
7244:       setEditing(null); setEditRows([]); setDirty(false); load();
7245: 
7246:     } catch (e: any) { alertMsg('❌ خطا', e?.message || String(e)); }
7247:     finally { savingRef.current = false; setSaving(false); }
7248: 
7249:   };
7250: 
7251: 
7252: 
7253:   const remove = async (inv: string, type: string) => {
7254:     const ok = await confirmMsg('حذف', 'فاکتور ' + inv + ' حذف شود؟');
7255:     if (!ok) return;
7256:     try {
7257:       if (type === 'sales') {
7258:         await deleteSale(inv);
7259:       } else {
7260:         await deletePurchase(inv);
7261:       }
7262:       showToast('✅ حذف شد');
7263:       load();
7264:     } catch (e: any) { showToast(e.message || 'خطا در حذف', true); }
7265:   };
7266: 
7267: 
7268: 
7269:   // ═══ حالت ویرایش ═══
7270: 
7271:     if (showInvoice) return (
7272:     <View style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden' }}><InvoiceView
7273:       invoice={showInvoice}
7274:       onBack={() => { setShowInvoice(null); load(); }}
7275:       onNew={() => { setShowInvoice(null); setEditing(null); setEditRows([]); setDirty(false); }}
7276:       showToast={showToast}
7277:     /></View>
7278:   );
7279: 
7280:   if (editing) return (
7281: 
7282:     <View>
7283:       <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 60 }} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
7284: 
7285:         <View style={[s.mgrHead, { backgroundColor: '#c0392b' }]}>
7286: 
7287:           <TouchableOpacity onPress={async () => { if (dirty) { const ok = await confirmMsg('خروج', 'تغییرات ذخیره نشده — خارج شوی؟'); if (!ok) return; } setEditing(null); setEditRows([]); setDirty(false); }}>
7288: 
7289:             <EL textKey="← بازگشت" style={{ color: '#fff', fontSize: 16 }} />
7290: 
7291:           </TouchableOpacity>
7292: 
7293:           <View style={{ flex: 1, marginRight: 10 }}>
7294: 
7295:             <Text style={s.mgrHeadTxt}>✏️ {editing.invoice}</Text>
7296: 
7297:             <Text style={{ color: '#fff', fontSize: 11, opacity: 0.9 }}>{editing.name} | {editing.code} | {toFaNum(editRows.length)} ردیف</Text>
7298: 
7299:           </View>
7300: 
7301:         </View>
7302: 
7303: 
7304: 
7305:         <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator>
7306: 
7307:           <View>
7308: 
7309:             <View style={[s.tblHeader, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse' }]}>
7310: 
7311:               <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 36, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 'bold', textAlign: 'center' }]}>#</Text>
7312: 
7313:               <EL textKey="کد" style={[s.thCell, { width: 90, textAlign: 'center' }]} />
7314: 
7315:               <EL textKey="نام مدل" style={[s.thCell, { width: 180, textAlign: 'center' }]} />
7316: 
7317:               <EL textKey="تعداد" style={[s.thCell, { width: 80, textAlign: 'center' }]} />
7318: 
7319:               <EL textKey="قیمت" style={[s.thCell, { width: 110, textAlign: 'center' }]} />
7320: 
7321:               <EL textKey="مبلغ" style={[s.thCell, { width: 100, textAlign: 'center' }]} />
7322: 
7323:               <EL textKey="پرداخت" style={[s.thCell, { width: 100, textAlign: 'center' }]} />
7324: 
7325:               <EL textKey="مانده" style={[s.thCell, { width: 100, textAlign: 'center' }]} />
7326: 
7327:              <EL textKey="تاریخ واریز" style={[s.thCell, { width: 340, textAlign: 'center' }]} />
7328: 
7329:               <EL textKey="بانک" style={[s.thCell, { width: 100, textAlign: 'center' }]} />
7330: 
7331:               <EL textKey="صاحب حساب" style={[s.thCell, { width: 130, textAlign: 'center' }]} />
7332: 
7333:               <EL textKey="کد صاحب حساب" style={[s.thCell, { width: 110, textAlign: 'center' }]} />
7334: 
7335:               <EL textKey="شرح" style={[s.thCell, { width: 140, textAlign: 'center' }]} />
7336: 
7337:               {editing.type === 'sales' && <EL textKey="باربری" style={[s.thCell, { width: 130, textAlign: 'center' }]} />}
7338: 
7339:               <EL textKey="حذف" style={[s.thCell, { width: 56, textAlign: 'center' }]} />
7340: 
7341:             </View>
7342: 
7343:             {editRows.map((r, i) => {
7344: 
7345:               const lt = (Number(r.quantity) || 0) * (Number(r.priceUnit) || 0);
7346: 
7347:               const lb = lt - (Number(r.payment) || 0);
7348: 
7349:               return (
7350: 
7351:                 <View key={i} style={[s.tblRow, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse' }, i % 2 === 0 && { backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }, r._deleted && { opacity: 0.4 }, r._new && { backgroundColor: C.isDark ? '#2e1065' : '#f5f3ff' }]}>
7352: 
7353:                   <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 36, fontSize: 12, color: '#d4af37', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{i + 1}</Text>
7354: 
7355:                   <View style={{ width: 90, paddingHorizontal: 3 }}><TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={r.modelCode} onChangeText={(v) => updRow(i, 'modelCode', v)} editable={!r._deleted} /></View>
7356: 
7357:                                   <View style={{ width: 180, paddingHorizontal: 3 }}>
7358: 
7359:                     <CellAutocomplete
7360: 
7361:                       value={r.modelName}
7362: 
7363:                       onChange={(v: string) => updRow(i, 'modelName', v)}
7364: 
7365:                       onSelect={(v: string) => {
7366: 
7367:                         const p = prods.find((x: any) => x.name === v);
7368: 
7369:                         if (p) {
7370: 
7371:                           updRow(i, 'modelCode', p.code || '');
7372: 
7373:                           updRow(i, 'priceUnit', p.price || 0);
7374: 
7375:                         }
7376: 
7377:                       }}
7378: 
7379:                       options={prods.map(p => p.name).filter(Boolean)}
7380: 
7381:                       placeholder={term('product') + '...'}
7382: 
7383:                     />
7384: 
7385:                   </View>
7386: 
7387:                   <View style={{ width: 80, paddingHorizontal: 3 }}><TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(r.quantity || '')} onChangeText={(v) => updRow(i, 'quantity', parseNum(v))} keyboardType="numeric" editable={!r._deleted} /></View>
7388: 
7389:                   <View style={{ width: 110, paddingHorizontal: 3 }}><TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(r.priceUnit || '')} onChangeText={(v) => updRow(i, 'priceUnit', parseNum(v))} keyboardType="numeric" editable={!r._deleted} /></View>
7390: 
7391:                   <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 100, fontSize: 12, color: '#00ff88', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{lt ? fmt(lt) : '—'}</Text>
7392: 
7393:                   <View style={{ width: 100, paddingHorizontal: 3 }}><TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(r.payment || '')} onChangeText={(v) => updRow(i, 'payment', parseNum(v))} keyboardType="numeric" editable={!r._deleted} /></View>
7394: 
7395:                   <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 100, fontSize: 12, color: lb > 0 ? '#dc2626' : '#059669', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{lt ? fmt(lb) : '—'}</Text>
7396: 
7397:                                   <View style={{ width: 340, paddingHorizontal: 3 }}><DateField value={r.depositDate} onChange={(v: string) => updRow(i, 'depositDate', v)} compact /></View>
7398: 
7399:                                                      <View style={{ width: 100, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
7400:                     <CellAutocomplete
7401:                       value={r.bankName}
7402:                       onChange={(v: string) => updRow(i, 'bankName', v)}
7403:                       options={banksList.map((b: any) => b.name)}
7404:                       placeholder="بانک"
7405:                     />
7406:                   </View>
7407: 
7408:                                   <View style={{ width: 130, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
7409:                     <CellAutocomplete
7410:                       value={r.accountHolder}
7411:                       onChange={(v: string) => updRow(i, 'accountHolder', v)}
7412:                       onSelect={async (v: string) => {
7413:                         try {
7414:                           const f = await lookupPayerUniversal(v);
7415:                           if (f) {
7416:                             updRow(i, 'accountHolderCode', f.code || '');
7417:                             if (f.bank) updRow(i, 'bankName', f.bank);
7418:                           } else {
7419:                             const code = await lookupCodeByName(v);
7420:                             updRow(i, 'accountHolderCode', code);
7421:                           }
7422:                         } catch {}
7423:                       }}
7424:                       options={Array.from(new Set([
7425:                         ...partiesList.map((p:any)=>p.name).filter(Boolean),
7426:                         ...partnersList.map((p:any)=>p.name).filter(Boolean),
7427:                         ...prods.map((p: any) => p.supplier_name || '').filter(Boolean)
7428:                       ]))}
7429:                       placeholder="صاحب حساب"
7430:                     />
7431:                   </View>
7432: 
7433:                   <View style={{ width: 110, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
7434:                     <TextInput accessibilityLabel="خودکار"
7435:                       style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.cardAlt, color: '#059669', borderColor: C.border, fontWeight: 'bold', textAlign: 'center', height: 34, fontSize: 11, paddingVertical: 0 }]}
7436:                       value={r.accountHolderCode || ''}
7437:                       editable={false}
7438:                       placeholder="خودکار"
7439:                     />
7440:                   </View>
7441: 
7442:                   <View style={{ width: 140, paddingHorizontal: 3 }}><TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={r.description} onChangeText={(v) => updRow(i, 'description', v)} editable={!r._deleted} /></View>
7443: 
7444:                   {editing.type === 'sales' && (
7445: 
7446:                                       <View style={{ width: 130, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
7447:                     <CellAutocomplete
7448:                       value={r.shipping}
7449:                       onChange={(v: string) => updRow(i, 'shipping', v)}
7450:                       options={shippingsList.map((s: any) => s.name)}
7451:                       placeholder="باربری"
7452:                     />
7453:                   </View>
7454: 
7455:                   )}
7456: 
7457:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => delRow(i)} style={[s.tdCell, { width: 56, alignItems: 'center' }]}><Text style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>{r._deleted ? '↺' : '🗑'}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
7458: 
7459:                 </View>
7460: 
7461:               );
7462: 
7463:             })}
7464: 
7465:           </View>
7466: 
7467:         </ScrollView>
7468: 
7469: 
7470: 
7471:         <TouchableOpacity style={s.addBtn2} onPress={addRow}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>{t('➕ افزودن ردیف')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
7472: 
7473:         <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, marginTop: 16 }}>
7474: 
7475:           <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#64748b' }]} onPress={() => { setEditing(null); setEditRows([]); }}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>{t('↩️ برگشت')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
7476: 
7477:           <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#8e44ad' }]} onPress={previewInvoice}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>{t('🖨️ صدور مجدد')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
7478:           <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { flex: 2, backgroundColor: '#059669' }]} onPress={save} disabled={saving}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>{t('💾 ذخیره')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
7479: 
7480:         </View>
7481: 
7482:       </ScrollView>
7483: 
7484:     </View>
7485: 
7486:   );

7493:

7495:

7497: 
7498: 
7499: 
7500:   return (
7501: 
7502:     <View>
7503: 
7504:       <TextInput accessibilityLabel="🔍 فاکتور، نام، تلفن..." style={[s.inp, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={q} onChangeText={setQ} placeholder="🔍 فاکتور، نام، تلفن..." placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
7505: 
7506:       <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6, marginVertical: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
7507: 
7508:         {[{ k: 'all', l: '📋 همه' }, { k: 'sales', l: '🛒 فروش' }, { k: 'purchases', l: '🛍️ خرید' }].map((f) => (
7509: 
7510:           <TouchableOpacity key={f.k} onPress={() => setFilter(f.k)} style={[s.chip, filter === f.k && s.chipActive]}><Text style={[s.chipTxt, filter === f.k && s.chipTxtActive]}>{f.l}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
7511: 
7512:         ))}
7513: 
7514:       </View>
7515:

7517: 
7518:         {[{ k: 'all', l: 'همه وضعیت' }, { k: 'paid', l: '✅ پرداخت‌شده' }, { k: 'unpaid', l: '❌ نشده' }, { k: 'partial', l: '⏳ جزئی' }].map((f) => (
7519: 
7520:           <TouchableOpacity key={f.k} onPress={() => setStatusFilter(f.k)} style={[s.chip, statusFilter === f.k && { backgroundColor: '#6c3483', borderColor: '#6c3483' }]}><Text style={[s.chipTxt, statusFilter === f.k && s.chipTxtActive]}>{f.l}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
7521: 
7522:         ))}
7523:

7527:

7536:           <View style={[s.tblHeader, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse' }]}>
7537: 
7538:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 40, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>#</Text>
7539: 
7540:             <EL textKey="فاکتور" style={[s.thCell, { width: 150 }]} />
7541: 
7542:             <EL textKey="نوع" style={[s.thCell, { width: 100 }]} />
7543: 
7544:             <EL textKey="نام" style={[s.thCell, { width: 150 }]} />
7545: 
7546:             <EL textKey="تلفن" style={[s.thCell, { width: 120 }]} />
7547: 
7548:             <EL textKey="تاریخ" style={[s.thCell, { width: 130 }]} />
7549: 
7550:             <EL textKey="ردیف" style={[s.thCell, { width: 70 }]} />
7551: 
7552:             <EL textKey="مبلغ" style={[s.thCell, { width: 130 }]} />
7553: 
7554:             <EL textKey="پرداخت" style={[s.thCell, { width: 130 }]} />
7555:

7557:

7559: 
7560:             <EL textKey="عملیات" style={[s.thCell, { width: 120 }]} />
7561: 
7562:           </View>
7563: 
7564:           {loading ? <ActivityIndicator color="#d4af37" style={{ marginTop: 20 }} /> : filtered.slice(0, 200).map((r: any, i: number) => (
7565: 
7566:             <View key={i} style={[s.tblRow, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse' }, i % 2 === 0 && { backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }]}>
7567:

7575: 
7576:               </View>

7579:

7581:

7583:

7585:

7587:

7589:

7591:

7595: 
7596:               </View>

7601: 
7602:                 <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => remove(r.invoice, r.type)} style={{ backgroundColor: '#fee2e2', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>🗑</Text></TouchableOpacity>
7603: 
7604:               </View>
7605: 
7606:             </View>
7607: 
7608:           ))}
7609: 
7610:         </View>

7615:

7626: //  REPORTS SECTION
7627: 
7628: // ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
7629: 
7630: function ReportsSection({ showToast }: any) {
7631: 
7632:   const [type, setType] = useState('debtors');
7633: 
7634:   const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);
7635: 
7636:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
7637: 
7638: 
7639: 
7640:   const load = async () => {
7641: 
7642:     setLoading(true);
7643: 
7644:     try {
7645: 
7646:       const [{ data: sales }, { data: purchases }] = await Promise.all([
7647: 
7648:         supabase.from('sales').select('*'),
7649: 
7650:         supabase.from('purchases').select('*'),
7651: 
7652:       ]);
7653: 
7654: 
7655: 
7656:       if (type === 'debtors') {
7657: 
7658:         const map: any = {};
7659: 
7660:         (sales || []).forEach((r: any) => {
7661: 
7662:           const code = r.customer_code || r.customer_phone;
7663: 
7664:           if (!code) return;
7665: 
7666:           if (!map[code]) map[code] = { code, name: r.customer_name, phone: r.customer_phone, total: 0, paid: 0 };
7667: 
7668:           map[code].total += (Number(r.quantity) || 0) * (Number(r.price_unit) || 0);
7669: 
7670:           map[code].paid += Number(r.payment) || 0;
7671: 
7672:         });
7673: 
7674:         const rows = Object.values(map).map((m: any) => ({ ...m, balance: m.total - m.paid })).filter((r: any) => r.balance > 0);
7675: 
7676:         rows.sort((a: any, b: any) => b.balance - a.balance);
7677: 
7678:         setData({ rows, total: rows.reduce((a: number, r: any) => a + r.balance, 0), title: 'بدهکاران', columns: ['کد', 'نام', 'تلفن', 'جمع', 'پرداخت', 'بدهی'] });
7679: 
7680:       } else if (type === 'creditors') {
7681: 
7682:         const map: any = {};
7683: 
7684:         (purchases || []).forEach((r: any) => {
7685: 
7686:           const code = r.supplier_code || r.supplier_phone;
7687: 
7688:           if (!code) return;
7689: 
7690:           if (!map[code]) map[code] = { code, name: r.supplier_name, phone: r.supplier_phone, total: 0, paid: 0 };
7691: 
7692:           map[code].total += (Number(r.quantity) || 0) * (Number(r.price_unit) || 0);
7693: 
7694:           map[code].paid += Number(r.payment) || 0;
7695: 
7696:         });
7697: 
7698:         const rows = Object.values(map).map((m: any) => ({ ...m, balance: m.total - m.paid })).filter((r: any) => r.balance > 0);
7699: 
7700:         rows.sort((a: any, b: any) => b.balance - a.balance);
7701: 
7702:         setData({ rows, total: rows.reduce((a: number, r: any) => a + r.balance, 0), title: 'طلبکاران', columns: ['کد', 'نام', 'تلفن', 'جمع', 'پرداخت', 'طلب'] });
7703: 
7704:       } else if (type === 'sales24h') {
7705: 
7706:         const cutoff = Date.now() - 24 * 3600 * 1000;
7707: 
7708:         const rows = (sales || []).filter((r: any) => { const d = parseDateAny(r.date_reg || r.date_factor); return d && d.getTime() >= cutoff; });
7709: 
7710:         setData({ rows, total: rows.reduce((a: number, r: any) => a + (Number(r.quantity) || 0) * (Number(r.price_unit) || 0), 0), title: 'فروش ۲۴ ساعت', columns: ['فاکتور', 'نام', 'تلفن', 'مبلغ', 'پرداخت'] });
7711: 
7712:       } else if (type === 'purchases24h') {
7713: 
7714:         const cutoff = Date.now() - 24 * 3600 * 1000;
7715: 
7716:         const rows = (purchases || []).filter((r: any) => { const d = parseDateAny(r.date_reg || r.date_factor); return d && d.getTime() >= cutoff; });
7717: 
7718:         setData({ rows, total: rows.reduce((a: number, r: any) => a + (Number(r.quantity) || 0) * (Number(r.price_unit) || 0), 0), title: 'خرید ۲۴ ساعت', columns: ['فاکتور', 'نام', 'تلفن', 'مبلغ', 'پرداخت'] });
7719: 
7720:       } else if (type === 'inventory') {
7721: 
7722:         const d = await getInventory();
7723: 
7724:         const rows = d.list.filter((m: any) => m.currentQty !== 0);
7725: 
7726:         setData({ rows, total: rows.reduce((a: number, r: any) => a + r.currentQty, 0), title: 'موجودی انبار', columns: ['کد', 'نام', 'خرید', 'فروش', 'موجودی', 'قفسه'] });
7727: 
7728:       }
7729: 
7730:     } catch (e: any) { showToast(e.message, true); }
7731: 
7732:     finally { setLoading(false); }
7733: 
7734:   };
7735: 
7736: 
7737: 
7738:   useEffect(() => { load(); }, [type]);
7739: 
7740: 
7741: 
7742:   const exportExcel = async () => {
7743: 
7744:     if (!data?.rows?.length) return;
7745: 
7746:     try {
7747: 
7748:       const ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(data.rows);
7749: 
7750:       const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
7751: 
7752:       XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, 'گزارش');
7753: 
7754:       const wbout = XLSX.write(wb, { type: 'base64', bookType: 'xlsx' });
7755: 
7756:       const path = (FileSystem as any).cacheDirectory + `report-${type}-${Date.now()}.xlsx`;
7757: 
7758:       await FileSystem.writeAsStringAsync(path, wbout, { encoding: 'base64' });
7759: 
7760:       if (await Sharing.isAvailableAsync()) await Sharing.shareAsync(path);
7761: 
7762:       showToast('✅ اکسل آماده شد');
7763: 
7764:     } catch (e: any) { showToast(e.message, true); }
7765: 
7766:   };
7767: 
7768: 
7769: 
7770:   return (
7771: 
7772:     <View>
7773: 
7774:       <EL text="{t('📊 نوع گزارش')}" style={s.secT} />
7775: 
7776:       <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
7777: 
7778:         {[
7779: 
7780:           { k: 'debtors', l: '📊 بدهکاران' },
7781: 
7782:           { k: 'creditors', l: '📊 طلبکاران' },
7783: 
7784:           { k: 'sales24h', l: '📈 فروش ۲۴س' },
7785: 
7786:           { k: 'purchases24h', l: '📉 خرید ۲۴س' },
7787: 
7788:           { k: 'inventory', l: '📦 موجودی' },
7789: 
7790:         ].map((f) => (
7791: 
7792:           <TouchableOpacity key={f.k} onPress={() => setType(f.k)} style={[s.chip, type === f.k && s.chipActive]}>
7793: 
7794:             <Text style={[s.chipTxt, type === f.k && s.chipTxtActive]}>{f.l}</Text>
7795: 
7796:           </TouchableOpacity>
7797: 
7798:         ))}
7799: 
7800:       </View>
7801: 
7802: 
7803: 
7804:       {loading ? <ActivityIndicator color="#d4af37" style={{ marginTop: 20 }} /> : data && (
7805: 
7806:         <View style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
7807: 
7808:           <View style={s.statsCard}>
7809: 
7810:             <Text style={s.statsLbl}>{data.title}</Text>
7811: 
7812:             <Text style={s.statsVal}>{fmt(data.total)}</Text>
7813: 
7814:             <Text style={s.statsUnit}>{toFaNum(data.rows.length)} مورد</Text>
7815: 
7816:           </View>
7817: 
7818: 
7819: 
7820:           <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator>
7821: 
7822:             <View>
7823: 
7824:               <View style={[s.tblHeader, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse' }]}>
7825: 
7826:                 <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 40, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>#</Text>
7827: 
7828:                 {data.columns.map((c: string, j: number) => (
7829: 
7830:                   <Text key={j} style={[s.thCell, { width: 140 }]}>{c}</Text>
7831: 
7832:                 ))}
7833: 
7834:               </View>
7835: 
7836:               {data.rows.slice(0, 200).map((r: any, i: number) => (
7837: 
7838:                 <View key={i} style={[s.tblRow, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse' }, i % 2 === 0 && { backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }]}>
7839: 
7840:                   <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 40, fontSize: 12, color: '#d4af37', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{i + 1}</Text>
7841: 
7842:                   {type === 'debtors' && <>
7843: 
7844:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: '#7c3aed', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{r.code}</Text>
7845: 
7846:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: C.text, textAlign: 'right' }]} numberOfLines={1}>{r.name}</Text>
7847: 
7848:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: C.textMut }]}>{r.phone || '—'}</Text>
7849: 
7850:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: '#00ff88', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{fmt(r.total)}</Text>
7851: 
7852:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: '#059669', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{fmt(r.paid)}</Text>
7853: 
7854:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: '#dc2626', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{fmt(r.balance)}</Text>
7855: 
7856:                   </>}
7857: 
7858:                   {type === 'creditors' && <>
7859: 
7860:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: '#7c3aed', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{r.code}</Text>
7861: 
7862:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: C.text, textAlign: 'right' }]} numberOfLines={1}>{r.name}</Text>
7863: 
7864:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: C.textMut }]}>{r.phone || '—'}</Text>
7865: 
7866:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: '#00ff88', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{fmt(r.total)}</Text>
7867: 
7868:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: '#059669', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{fmt(r.paid)}</Text>
7869: 
7870:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: '#dc2626', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{fmt(r.balance)}</Text>
7871: 
7872:                   </>}
7873: 
7874:                   {(type === 'sales24h' || type === 'purchases24h') && <>
7875: 
7876:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: '#7c3aed', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{r.invoice_number}</Text>
7877: 
7878:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: C.text, textAlign: 'right' }]} numberOfLines={1}>{r.customer_name || r.supplier_name}</Text>
7879: 
7880:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: C.textMut }]}>{r.customer_phone || r.supplier_phone || '—'}</Text>
7881: 
7882:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: '#00ff88', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{fmt((Number(r.quantity) || 0) * (Number(r.price_unit) || 0))}</Text>
7883: 
7884:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: '#059669', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{fmt(r.payment || 0)}</Text>
7885: 
7886:                   </>}
7887: 
7888:                   {type === 'inventory' && <>
7889: 
7890:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: '#7c3aed', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{r.code}</Text>
7891: 
7892:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: C.text, textAlign: 'right' }]} numberOfLines={1}>{r.name}</Text>
7893: 
7894:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: C.text }]}>{fmt(r.bought)}</Text>
7895: 
7896:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: C.text }]}>{fmt(r.sold)}</Text>
7897: 
7898:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: r.currentQty < 0 ? '#dc2626' : '#00ff88', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{fmt(r.currentQty)}</Text>
7899: 
7900:                     <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 140, fontSize: 12, color: C.textMut }]}>{r.shelf || '—'}</Text>
7901: 
7902:                   </>}
7903: 
7904:                 </View>
7905: 
7906:               ))}
7907: 
7908:             </View>
7909: 
7910:           </ScrollView>
7911: 
7912: 
7913: 
7914:           <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, marginTop: 12 }}>
7915: 
7916:             <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#c0392b' }]} onPress={exportExcel}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>{t('📥 اکسل')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
7917: 
7918:             <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#059669' }]} onPress={load}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>{t('🔄 بروزرسانی')}</Text></TouchableOpacity>
7919: 
7920:           </View>
7921: 
7922:         </View>
7923: 
7924:       )}
7925: 
7926:     </View>
7927: 
7928:   );
7929: 
7930: }
7931:

7944: function PresetSection({ showToast }: any) {
7945: 
7946:   const [current, setCurrent] = useState<string>(getPreset());
7947: 
7948: 
7949: 
7950:   const applyPreset = async (k: string) => {
7951: 
7952:     try {
7953: 
7954:       await setPreset(k as any);
7955: 
7956:       setCurrent(k);
7957: 
7958:       showToast('✅ ' + (PRESETS as any)[k].name);
7959: 
7960:       if ((globalThis as any).__reloadPreset) (globalThis as any).__reloadPreset();
7961: 
7962:     } catch (e: any) {
7963: 
7964:       showToast(e?.message || 'خطا', true);
7965: 
7966:     }
7967: 
7968:   };
7969: 
7970: 
7971: 
7972:   return (
7973: 
7974:     <View>
7975: 
7976:       <EL text="🎨 نوع کسب‌وکار" style={s.formTitle} />
7977: 
7978:       <Text style={[s.lbl, { color: C.textMut, textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: 16 }]}>
7979: 
7980:         با انتخاب هرکدام، واژه‌های برنامه تغییر می‌کنند
7981: 
7982:       </Text>
7983: 
7984: 
7985: 
7986:       {Object.values(PRESETS).map((p: any) => {
7987: 
7988:         const active = current === p.key;
7989: 
7990:         return (
7991: 
7992:           <TouchableOpacity
7993: 
7994:             key={p.key}
7995: 
7996:             onPress={() => applyPreset(p.key)}
7997: 
7998:             style={[s.customerCard, {
7999: 
8000:               backgroundColor: active ? (C.isDark ? '#064e3b' : '#ecfdf5') : C.card,
8001: 
8002:               borderColor: active ? '#059669' : C.border,
8003: 
8004:               borderWidth: 2,
8005: 
8006:               marginBottom: 10,
8007: 
8008:             }]}
8009: 
8010:           >
8011: 
8012:             <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row-reverse', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12 }}>
8013: 
8014:               <Text style={{ fontSize: 34 }}>{p.icon}</Text>
8015: 
8016:               <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
8017: 
8018:                 <Text style={[s.customerCardTitle, {
8019: 
8020:                   color: C.text,
8021: 
8022:                   borderBottomWidth: 0,
8023: 
8024:                   marginBottom: 0,
8025: 
8026:                   paddingBottom: 0,
8027: 
8028:                 }]}>
8029: 
8030:                   {p.name}
8031: 
8032:                 </Text>
8033: 
8034:                 <Text style={{ color: C.textMut, fontSize: 11, marginTop: 4, textAlign: 'right' }}>
8035: 
8036:                   {p.terms.product} • {p.terms.customer} • {p.terms.sale}
8037: 
8038:                 </Text>
8039: 
8040:               </View>
8041: 
8042:               {active ? <Text style={{ color: '#059669', fontSize: 22 }}>✅</Text> : null}
8043: 
8044:             </View>
8045: 
8046:           </TouchableOpacity>
8047: 
8048:         );
8049: 
8050:       })}
8051: 
8052: 
8053: 
8054:       <Text style={[s.lblS, { color: C.textMut, textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 12 }]}>
8055: 
8056:         💡 پس از انتخاب، برنامه خودکار دوباره بارگذاری می‌شود
8057: 
8058:       </Text>
8059: 
8060:     </View>
8061: 
8062:   );
8063: 
8064: }

8069: 
8070: function SettingsSection({ showToast, settings, setSettings, reload }: any) {
8071: 
8072:   const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
8073: 
8074:   const pins = settings.tab_pins || {};
8075: 
8076:   const [emailInput, setEmailInput] = useState('');
8077: 
8078:   const [autoDelEnabled, setAutoDelEnabled] = useState(false);
8079: 
8080:   const [autoDelHours, setAutoDelHours] = useState('1');
8081: 
8082:   const [schedDailyEnabled, setSchedDailyEnabled] = useState(false);
8083: 
8084:   const [schedDailyHour, setSchedDailyHour] = useState('21');
8085: 
8086:   const [schedDailyDays, setSchedDailyDays] = useState<string[]>([]);
8087: 
8088:   const [schedDailyType, setSchedDailyType] = useState('daily-combined');
8089: 
8090:   const [schedWeeklyEnabled, setSchedWeeklyEnabled] = useState(false);
8091: 
8092:   const [schedWeeklyDay, setSchedWeeklyDay] = useState('6');
8093: 
8094:   const [schedWeeklyHour, setSchedWeeklyHour] = useState('21');
8095: 
8096:   const [schedWeeklyType, setSchedWeeklyType] = useState('weekly-combined');
8097: 
8098: 
8099: 
8100:   useEffect(() => {
8101: 
8102:     if (!settings) return;
8103: 
8104:     setAutoDelEnabled(!!settings.auto_delete_enabled);
8105: 
8106:     setAutoDelHours(String(settings.auto_delete_hours || 1));
8107: 
8108:     const sd = settings.sched_daily || {};
8109: 
8110:     setSchedDailyEnabled(!!sd.enabled);
8111: 
8112:     setSchedDailyHour(String(sd.hour ?? 21));
8113: 
8114:     setSchedDailyDays(sd.days || []);
8115: 
8116:     setSchedDailyType(sd.type || 'daily-combined');
8117: 
8118:     const sw = settings.sched_weekly || {};
8119: 
8120:     setSchedWeeklyEnabled(!!sw.enabled);
8121: 
8122:     setSchedWeeklyDay(String(sw.day ?? 6));
8123: 
8124:     setSchedWeeklyHour(String(sw.hour ?? 21));
8125: 
8126:     setSchedWeeklyType(sw.type || 'weekly-combined');
8127: 
8128:   }, [settings]);
8129: 
8130: 
8131: 
8132:   const [lastBackupLabel, setLastBackupLabel] = useState<string | null>(null);
8133: 
8134:   useEffect(() => {
8135: 
8136:     (async () => {
8137: 
8138:       try {
8139: 
8140:         const last = await getLastAutoBackup();
8141: 
8142:         setLastBackupLabel(last);
8143: 
8144:       } catch (e) { console.log('[backup] read last err:', e); }
8145: 
8146:       try {
8147: 
8148:         await autoBackup();
8149: 
8150:         const last2 = await getLastAutoBackup();
8151: 
8152:         setLastBackupLabel(last2);
8153: 
8154:       } catch (e) { console.log('[backup] auto err:', e); }
8155: 
8156:     })();
8157: 
8158:   }, []);
8159: 
8160: 
8161: 
8162:   const save = async () => {
8163: 
8164:     setSaving(true);
8165: 
8166:     try {
8167: 
8168:       await updateSettings({
8169: 
8170:         ...settings,
8171: 
8172:         auto_delete_enabled: autoDelEnabled,
8173: 
8174:         auto_delete_hours: parseInt(autoDelHours, 10) || 1,
8175: 
8176:         sched_daily: { enabled: schedDailyEnabled, hour: parseInt(schedDailyHour, 10), days: schedDailyDays, type: schedDailyType },
8177: 
8178:         sched_weekly: { enabled: schedWeeklyEnabled, day: parseInt(schedWeeklyDay, 10), hour: parseInt(schedWeeklyHour, 10), type: schedWeeklyType },
8179: 
8180:       });
8181: 
8182:       const _sMode = getMode();
8183: 
8184:       alertMsg('✅ موفق', _sMode === 'offline' ? 'تنظیمات ذخیره شد و در صف آپلود قرار گرفت' : 'تنظیمات ذخیره شد');
8185: 
8186:       reload && reload();
8187: 
8188:     } catch (e: any) { alertMsg('❌ خطا', e?.message || String(e)); }
8189: 
8190:     finally { setSaving(false); }
8191: 
8192:   };
8193: 
8194: 
8195: 
8196:   const emails: string[] = settings.emails || [];
8197: 
8198:   const addEmail = () => {
8199: 
8200:     if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(emailInput)) return showToast('ایمیل معتبر وارد کن', true);
8201: 
8202:     setSettings({ ...settings, emails: [...emails, emailInput] });
8203: 
8204:     setEmailInput('');
8205: 
8206:   };
8207: 
8208:   const removeEmail = (i: number) => setSettings({ ...settings, emails: emails.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i) });
8209: 
8210:   const toggleDay = (d: string) => setSchedDailyDays((prev) => prev.includes(d) ? prev.filter((x) => x !== d) : [...prev, d]);
8211: 
8212:   return (
8213: 
8214:     <View>
8215: 
8216:       <EL text="{t('🌍 زبان برنامه')}" style={s.secT} />
8217: 
8218:       <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { backgroundColor: '#0ea5e9', marginBottom: 12 }]} onPress={() => { (globalThis as any).__openLang?.(); }}>
8219: 
8220:         <Text style={s.btnTxt}>{t('🌍 تغییر زبان / Change Language')}</Text>
8221: 
8222:       </TouchableOpacity>
8223: 
8224: 
8225: 
8226:       <EL text="{t('📅 نوع تقویم')}" style={s.secT} />
8227: 
8228:       <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
8229: 
8230:         {[{ k: 'jalali', l: '🌙 شمسی' }, { k: 'gregorian', l: '🌍 میلادی' }, { k: 'hijri', l: '🕋 قمری' }].map((f) => (
8231: 
8232:           <TouchableOpacity key={f.k} onPress={() => { setSettings({ ...settings, cal_type: f.k as CalType }); saveCalType(f.k as CalType); }} style={[s.chip, settings.cal_type === f.k && s.chipActive]}>
8233: 
8234:             <Text style={[s.chipTxt, settings.cal_type === f.k && s.chipTxtActive]}>{f.l}</Text>
8235: 
8236:           </TouchableOpacity>
8237: 
8238:         ))}
8239: 
8240:       </View>
8241: 
8242: 
8243: 
8244:       <EL text="💱 واحد پول" style={s.secT} />
8245:       <Text style={{ color: C.textMut, fontSize: 11, textAlign: 'right', marginBottom: 6 }}>
8246:         واحد پول پیش‌فرض کل برنامه — همه مبالغ با این واحد نمایش داده می‌شوند
8247:       </Text>
8248:       <CurrencyField
8249:         value={settings.currency_code || 'AFN'}
8250:         onChange={(code: string, cur: any) => {
8251:           setSettings({
8252:             ...settings,
8253:             currency_code: cur.code,

8258:           });
8259:         }}
8260:       />
8261: 
8262:       <EL text="💼 تنظیمات حقوق و دستمزد" style={s.secT} />
8263:       <Text style={{ color: C.textMut, fontSize: 11, textAlign: 'right', marginBottom: 8 }}>
8264:         کنترل کامل کسورات و ضرایب — همه در گوشی و سرور ذخیره می‌شوند
8265:       </Text>
8266: 
8267:       {/* کسر غیبت */}
8268:       <View style={s.rowBetween}>
8269:         <Text style={[s.lbl, { color: C.textMut }]}>📉 کسر غیبت</Text>

8271:       </View>

8273:       <TextInput style={[s.inp, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(settings.payroll_absence_rate ?? 1)} onChangeText={(v) => setSettings({ ...settings, payroll_absence_rate: parseFloat(v) || 0 })} keyboardType="decimal-pad" />
8274: 
8275:       {/* کسر مرخصی بدون حقوق */}
8276:       <View style={s.rowBetween}>
8277:         <Text style={[s.lbl, { color: C.textMut }]}>📉 کسر مرخصی بدون حقوق</Text>
8278:         <Switch value={!!settings.payroll_deduct_unpaid_leave} onValueChange={(v) => setSettings({ ...settings, payroll_deduct_unpaid_leave: v })} />
8279:       </View>
8280:       <Text style={[s.lblS, { color: C.textMut }]}>ضریب مرخصی بدون حقوق</Text>

8436:

8438: 
8439:       <EL textKey="روز هفته" style={[s.lblS, { color: C.textMut }]} />
8440: 
8441:       <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
8442:

8460:

8462:

8518:

8544:

8598:

8602:

8606:

8624: 
8625: //  SOURCES SECTION — جدول ۹ ستونه قابل ویرایش
8626:
