8628: 
8629: 
8630: function PartiesSection({ showToast }: any) {
8631:   const [list, setList] = useState<any[]>([]);
8632:   const [q, setQ] = useState('');
8633:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
8634:   const [editing, setEditing] = useState<any>(null);
8635:   const [drafts, setDrafts] = useState<any[]>([]);
8636: 
8637:   const load = async () => {
8638:     setLoading(true);
8639:     try {
8640:       const data = await getParties();
8641:       const sorted = [...(data || [])].sort((a: any, b: any) => {
8642:         const ca = String(a.code || '');
8643:         const cb = String(b.code || '');
8644:         if (!ca && !cb) return 0;
8645:         if (!ca) return 1;
8646:         if (!cb) return -1;
8647:         return ca.localeCompare(cb, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
8648:       });
8649:       setList(sorted);
8650:     } catch {}
8651:     finally { setLoading(false); }
8652:   };
8653:   useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);
8654: 
8655:   const filtered = q
8656:     ? list.filter((p: any) => (String(p.code || '') + ' ' + String(p.name || '') + ' ' + String(p.phone || '')).toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()))
8657:     : list;
8658: 
8659:   const typeLabel = (t: string) => t === 'supplier' ? 'تأمین‌کننده' : t === 'customer' ? 'مشتری' : 'هر دو';
8660:   const typeColor = (t: string) => t === 'supplier' ? '#ea580c' : t === 'customer' ? '#059669' : '#7c3aed';
8661: 
8662:   const getNextPartyCode = (listData: any[], draftData: any[], excludeIdx: number = -1) => {
8663:     const codes = [
8664:       ...listData.map(x => String(x.code || '')),
8665:       ...draftData.filter((_, i) => i !== excludeIdx).map(x => String(x.code || ''))
8666:     ].filter(Boolean);
8667:     if (!codes.length) return '';
8668:     let best: any = null;
8669:     codes.forEach(c => {
8670:       const m = c.match(/^(.*?)(\d+)$/);
8671:       if (m) {
8672:         const n = parseInt(m[2], 10);
8673:         if (!best || n > best.n) best = { prefix: m[1], n, width: m[2].length };
8674:       }
8675:     });
8676:     if (best) return best.prefix + String(best.n + 1).padStart(best.width, '0');
8677:     return codes[codes.length - 1] + '-2';
8678:   };
8679: 
8680:   const autoPartyCode = async (phoneArg?: string) => {
8681:     if (!editing) return;
8682:     const phone = phoneArg !== undefined ? phoneArg : (editing.phone || '');
8683:     const p = String(phone).replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
8684:     if (!p) return;
8685:     const existing = list.find((x: any) => String(x.phone || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '') === p);
8686:     if (existing && existing.code) {
8687:       setEditing((prev: any) => prev ? { ...prev, code: existing.code, phone } : prev);
8688:       showToast('⚠️ شماره قبلاً برای «' + (existing.name || '') + '» ثبت شده؛ کد قبلی درج شد', true);
8689:       return;
8690:     }
8691:     const next = getNextPartyCode(list, [], -1);
8692:     if (next) setEditing((prev: any) => prev ? { ...prev, code: next, phone } : prev);
8693:     else setEditing((prev: any) => prev ? { ...prev, phone } : prev);
8694:   };
8695: 
8696:   const autoDraftCode = (idx: number, phoneArg?: string) => {
8697:     const d = drafts[idx];
8698:     if (!d) return;
8699:     const phone = phoneArg !== undefined ? phoneArg : (d.phone || '');
8700:     const p = String(phone).replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
8701:     if (!p) return;
8702:     const existing = list.find((x: any) => String(x.phone || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '') === p);
8703:     if (existing && existing.code) {
8704:       updateDraft(idx, 'code', existing.code);
8705:       showToast('⚠️ شماره قبلاً برای «' + (existing.name || '') + '» ثبت شده؛ کد قبلی درج شد', true);
8706:       return;
8707:     }
8708:     const draftDup = drafts.find((x: any, i: number) => i !== idx && String(x.phone || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '') === p);
8709:     if (draftDup && draftDup.code) {
8710:       updateDraft(idx, 'code', draftDup.code);
8711:       showToast('⚠️ این شماره در ردیف دیگری ثبت شده؛ همان کد درج شد', true);
8712:       return;
8713:     }
8714:     const next = getNextPartyCode(list, drafts, idx);
8715:     if (next) updateDraft(idx, 'code', next);
8716:   };
8717: 
8718:   const save = async () => {
8719:     if (!editing) return;
8720:     if (!editing.name) { showToast('نام الزامی است', true); return; }
8721:     const dupName = list.find((x: any) => (x.local_id !== editing.local_id) && (x.id !== editing.id) && String(x.name || '').trim() === String(editing.name || '').trim());
8722:     if (dupName) { showToast('⚠️ این نام قبلاً ثبت شده', true); return; }
8723:     const phoneNorm = String(editing.phone || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
8724:     if (phoneNorm) {
8725:       const dupPhone = list.find((x: any) => (x.local_id !== editing.local_id) && (x.id !== editing.id) && String(x.phone || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '') === phoneNorm);
8726:       if (dupPhone) {
8727:         setEditing({ ...editing, code: dupPhone.code || editing.code });
8728:         showToast('⚠️ این شماره قبلاً ثبت شده؛ کد قبلی درج شد', true);
8729:         return;
8730:       }
8731:     }
8732:     try {
8733:       const payload: any = { ...editing };
8734:       delete payload.id;
8735:       if (editing.id || editing.local_id) await updateParty(editing.local_id || editing.id, payload);
8736:       else await createParty(payload);
8737:       showToast('✅ طرف‌حساب ذخیره شد');
8738:       setEditing(null);
8739:       load();
8740:     } catch (e: any) { showToast(e?.message || String(e), true); }
8741:   };
8742: 
8743:   const remove = async (p: any) => {
8744:     const ok = await confirmMsg('حذف طرف‌حساب', '«' + (p.name || '') + '» حذف شود؟');
8745:     if (!ok) return;
8746:     try { await deleteParty(p.local_id || p.id); load(); showToast('✅ حذف شد'); }
8747:     catch (e: any) { showToast(e?.message || String(e), true); }
8748:   };
8749: 
8750:   const startNew = () => {
8751:     setDrafts((prev: any[]) => [...prev, { local_id: '', code: '', name: '', type: 'supplier', phone: '', address: '', contact_name: '', note: '', status: 'active', opening_balance: 0 }]);
8752:   };
8753: 
8754:   const updateDraft = (idx: number, field: string, value: any) => {
8755:     setDrafts((prev: any[]) => prev.map((d, i) => i === idx ? { ...d, [field]: value } : d));
8756:   };
8757: 
8758:   const removeDraft = (idx: number) => {
8759:     setDrafts((prev: any[]) => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx));
8760:   };
8761: 
8762:   const saveDrafts = async () => {
8763:     if (!drafts.length) return;
8764:     for (const d of drafts) if (!d.name) { showToast('نام در همه ردیف‌ها الزامی است', true); return; }
8765: 
8766:     // چک تلفن تکراری
8767:     for (let i = 0; i < drafts.length; i++) {
8768:       const p = String(drafts[i].phone || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
8769:       if (!p) continue;
8770:       const existing = list.find((x: any) => String(x.phone || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '') === p);
8771:       if (existing) {
8772:         showToast('⚠️ شماره «' + p + '» قبلاً برای «' + (existing.name || '') + '» ثبت شده است', true);
8773:         return;
8774:       }
8775:       const otherDraft = drafts.find((x: any, j: number) => j !== i && String(x.phone || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '') === p);
8776:       if (otherDraft) {
8777:         showToast('⚠️ شماره «' + p + '» در ردیف‌های دیگر تکراری است', true);
8778:         return;
8779:       }
8780:     }
8781: 
8782:     const newDrafts = [...drafts];
8783:     for (let i = 0; i < newDrafts.length; i++) {
8784:       let d = newDrafts[i];
8785:       const p = String(d.phone || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '');
8786:       if (p) {
8787:         const existing = list.find((x: any) => String(x.phone || '').replace(/[^0-9]/g, '') === p);
8788:         if (existing && existing.code) d.code = existing.code;
8789:       }
8790:       if (!d.code) d.code = getNextPartyCode(list, newDrafts, i);
8791:     }
8792: 
8793:     let success = 0;
8794:     for (const d of newDrafts) {
8795:       try { await createParty(d); success++; }
8796:       catch (e: any) { showToast('خطا: ' + (e?.message || String(e)), true); }
8797:     }
8798:     if (success) {
8799:       showToast('✅ ' + success + ' طرف‌حساب ذخیره شد');
8800:       setDrafts([]);
8801:       load();
8802:     }
8803:   };
8804: 
8805:   return (
8806:     <View>
8807:       <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 6, marginBottom: 10 }}>
8808:         <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#7c3aed' }]} onPress={startNew}>
8809:           <Text style={s.btnTxt}>➕ افزودن ردیف</Text>
8810:         </TouchableOpacity>
8811:         {drafts.length > 0 && (
8812:           <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { backgroundColor: '#059669' }]} onPress={saveDrafts}>
8813:             <Text style={s.btnTxt}>💾 ذخیره همه ({drafts.length})</Text>
8814:           </TouchableOpacity>
8815:         )}
8816:       </View>
8817: 
8818:       <TextInput style={[s.inp, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={q} onChangeText={setQ} placeholder="🔍 جستجو در طرف‌حساب‌ها..." placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
8819:       <Text style={s.secT}>👥 طرف‌حساب‌ها ({toFaNum(filtered.length)})</Text>
8820: 
8821:       <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator>
8822:         <View>
8823:           <View style={[s.tblHeader, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse', position: 'relative', zIndex: 10 }]}>
8824:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 40 }]}>#</Text>
8825:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 180 }]}>نام</Text>
8826:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 130 }]}>تلفن</Text>
8827:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 120 }]}>کد</Text>
8828:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 110 }]}>نوع</Text>
8829:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 90 }]}>وضعیت</Text>
8830:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 200 }]}>آدرس</Text>
8831:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 130 }]}>شخص تماس</Text>
8832:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 160 }]}>یادداشت</Text>
8833:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 100 }]}>عملیات</Text>
8834:           </View>
8835: 
8836:           <ScrollView style={{ maxHeight: 420 }} nestedScrollEnabled>
8837:             {drafts.map((d, idx) => (
8838:               <View key={'draft-' + idx} style={[s.tblRow, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse', backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }]}>
8839:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 40, textAlign: 'center', color: '#d4af37', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>D{idx + 1}</Text>
8840:                 <View style={{ width: 180, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
8841:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(d.name || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => updateDraft(idx, 'name', v)} placeholder="نام *" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
8842:                 </View>
8843:                 <View style={{ width: 130, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
8844:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(d.phone || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => updateDraft(idx, 'phone', v)} onEndEditing={(e: any) => autoDraftCode(idx, e?.nativeEvent?.text)} onBlur={() => autoDraftCode(idx)} keyboardType="phone-pad" placeholder="تلفن" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
8845:                 </View>
8846:                 <View style={{ width: 120, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
8847:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(d.code || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => updateDraft(idx, 'code', v)} placeholder="کد" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
8848:                 </View>
8849:                 <View style={{ width: 110, flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 2 }}>
8850:                   {['supplier','customer','both'].map((t: string) => (
8851:                     <TouchableOpacity key={t} onPress={() => updateDraft(idx, 'type', t)} style={{ flex: 1, padding: 4, borderRadius: 4, backgroundColor: d.type === t ? typeColor(t) : '#e2e8f0', alignItems: 'center' }}>
8852:                       <Text style={{ color: d.type === t ? '#fff' : '#475569', fontSize: 8 }}>{typeLabel(t)}</Text>
8853:                     </TouchableOpacity>
8854:                   ))}
8855:                 </View>
8856:                 <View style={{ width: 90, flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 2 }}>
8857:                   {['active','inactive'].map((st: string) => (
8858:                     <TouchableOpacity key={st} onPress={() => updateDraft(idx, 'status', st)} style={{ flex: 1, padding: 4, borderRadius: 4, backgroundColor: d.status === st ? (st === 'active' ? '#059669' : '#dc2626') : '#e2e8f0', alignItems: 'center' }}>
8859:                       <Text style={{ color: d.status === st ? '#fff' : '#475569', fontSize: 8 }}>{st === 'active' ? 'فعال' : 'غیرفعال'}</Text>
8860:                     </TouchableOpacity>
8861:                   ))}
8862:                 </View>
8863:                 <View style={{ width: 200, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
8864:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(d.address || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => updateDraft(idx, 'address', v)} placeholder="آدرس" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
8865:                 </View>
8866:                 <View style={{ width: 130, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
8867:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(d.contact_name || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => updateDraft(idx, 'contact_name', v)} placeholder="شخص تماس" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
8868:                 </View>
8869:                 <View style={{ width: 160, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
8870:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(d.note || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => updateDraft(idx, 'note', v)} placeholder="یادداشت" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
8871:                 </View>
8872:                 <View style={{ width: 100, flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 4, justifyContent: 'center' }}>
8873:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => removeDraft(idx)} style={{ backgroundColor: '#fee2e2', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text style={{ color: '#fff', fontSize: 12 }}>✕</Text></TouchableOpacity>
8874:                 </View>
8875:               </View>
8876:             ))}
8877: 
8878:             {editing && (
8879:               <View style={[s.tblRow, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse', backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }]}>
8880:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 40 }]}>✏️</Text>
8881:                 <View style={{ width: 180, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
8882:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(editing.name || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => setEditing({ ...editing, name: v })} placeholder="نام *" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
8883:                 </View>
8884:                 <View style={{ width: 130, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
8885:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(editing.phone || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => setEditing({ ...editing, phone: v })} onEndEditing={() => autoPartyCode()} onBlur={() => autoPartyCode()} keyboardType="phone-pad" placeholder="تلفن" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
8886:                 </View>
8887:                 <View style={{ width: 120, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
8888:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(editing.code || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => setEditing({ ...editing, code: v })} placeholder="کد" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
8889:                 </View>
8890:                 <View style={{ width: 110, flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 2 }}>
8891:                   {['supplier','customer','both'].map((t: string) => (
8892:                     <TouchableOpacity key={t} onPress={() => setEditing({ ...editing, type: t })} style={{ flex: 1, padding: 4, borderRadius: 4, backgroundColor: editing.type === t ? typeColor(t) : '#e2e8f0', alignItems: 'center' }}>
8893:                       <Text style={{ color: editing.type === t ? '#fff' : '#475569', fontSize: 8 }}>{typeLabel(t)}</Text>
8894:                     </TouchableOpacity>
8895:                   ))}
8896:                 </View>
8897:                 <View style={{ width: 90, flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 2 }}>
8898:                   {['active','inactive'].map((st: string) => (
8899:                     <TouchableOpacity key={st} onPress={() => setEditing({ ...editing, status: st })} style={{ flex: 1, padding: 4, borderRadius: 4, backgroundColor: editing.status === st ? (st === 'active' ? '#059669' : '#dc2626') : '#e2e8f0', alignItems: 'center' }}>
8900:                       <Text style={{ color: editing.status === st ? '#fff' : '#475569', fontSize: 8 }}>{st === 'active' ? 'فعال' : 'غیرفعال'}</Text>
8901:                     </TouchableOpacity>
8902:                   ))}
8903:                 </View>
8904:                 <View style={{ width: 200, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
8905:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(editing.address || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => setEditing({ ...editing, address: v })} placeholder="آدرس" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
8906:                 </View>
8907:                 <View style={{ width: 130, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
8908:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(editing.contact_name || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => setEditing({ ...editing, contact_name: v })} placeholder="شخص تماس" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
8909:                 </View>
8910:                 <View style={{ width: 160, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
8911:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(editing.note || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => setEditing({ ...editing, note: v })} placeholder="یادداشت" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
8912:                 </View>
8913:                 <View style={{ width: 100, flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 4, justifyContent: 'center' }}>
8914:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={save} style={{ backgroundColor: '#059669', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text style={{ color: '#fff', fontSize: 12 }}>💾</Text></TouchableOpacity>
8915:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setEditing(null)} style={{ backgroundColor: '#64748b', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text style={{ color: '#fff', fontSize: 12 }}>✕</Text></TouchableOpacity>
8916:                 </View>
8917:               </View>
8918:             )}
8919: 
8920:             {loading ? <ActivityIndicator color="#d4af37" style={{ marginTop: 20 }} /> : filtered.map((p: any, i: number) => (
8921:               <View key={p.local_id || p.id || i} style={[s.tblRow, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse' }, i % 2 === 0 && { backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }]}>
8922:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 40, fontSize: 12, color: '#d4af37', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{i + 1}</Text>
8923:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 180, fontSize: 12, color: C.text, fontWeight: 'bold', textAlign: 'right' }]} numberOfLines={1}>{p.name || '—'}</Text>
8924:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 130, fontSize: 11, color: C.text }]}>{p.phone || '—'}</Text>
8925:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 120, fontSize: 11, color: '#7c3aed' }]}>{p.code || '—'}</Text>
8926:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 110, fontSize: 11, color: typeColor(p.type), fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{typeLabel(p.type)}</Text>
8927:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 90, fontSize: 11, color: p.status === 'inactive' ? '#dc2626' : '#059669', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{p.status === 'inactive' ? 'غیرفعال' : 'فعال'}</Text>
8928:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 200, fontSize: 10, color: C.textMut, textAlign: 'right' }]} numberOfLines={1}>{p.address || '—'}</Text>
8929:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 130, fontSize: 10, color: C.text }]}>{p.contact_name || '—'}</Text>
8930:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 160, fontSize: 10, color: C.textMut, textAlign: 'right' }]} numberOfLines={1}>{p.note || '—'}</Text>
8931:                 <View style={[s.tdCell, { width: 100, flexDirection: 'row', gap: 4, justifyContent: 'center' }]}>
8932:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setEditing({ ...p })} style={{ backgroundColor: '#dbeafe', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>✏️</Text></TouchableOpacity>
8933:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => remove(p)} style={{ backgroundColor: '#fee2e2', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>🗑</Text></TouchableOpacity>
8934:                 </View>
8935:               </View>
8936:             ))}
8937:           </ScrollView>
8938:         </View>
8939:       </ScrollView>
8940:     </View>
8941:   );
8942: }
8943: 
8944: function PartnersSection({ showToast }: any) {
8945:   const [banksList, setBanksList] = useState<any[]>([]);
8946:   useEffect(() => { getBanks().then(setBanksList).catch(()=>{}); }, []);
8947: 
8948:   const [list, setList] = useState<any[]>([]);
8949:   const [q, setQ] = useState('');
8950:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
8951:   const [editing, setEditing] = useState<any>(null);
8952:   const [drafts, setDrafts] = useState<any[]>([]);
8953:   const [bankPartner, setBankPartner] = useState<any>(null);
8954:   const [bankList, setBankList] = useState<any[]>([]);
8955:   const [bankDrafts, setBankDrafts] = useState<any[]>([]);
8956:   const [bankLoading, setBankLoading] = useState(false);
8957: 
8958:   const [txnPartner, setTxnPartner] = useState<any>(null);
8959:   const [txnList, setTxnList] = useState<any[]>([]);
8960:   const [txnBanks, setTxnBanks] = useState<any[]>([]);
8961:   const [txnLoading, setTxnLoading] = useState(false);
8962:   const [txnForm, setTxnForm] = useState<any>({
8963:     type: 'deposit', currency: 'AFN', amount: '', bank_account_local_id: '',
8964:     box_effect: 'none', box_id: '', box_currency: '', exchange_rate: 1,
8965:     to_partner_code: '', to_currency: 'AFN', to_amount: '', transaction_date: new Date().toISOString(), note: '',
8966:     account_type: 'current', counterpart_type: 'none', counterpart_code: ''
8967:   });
8968: 
8969:   const load = async () => {
8970:     setLoading(true);
8971:     try {
8972:       const data = await getPartners();
8973:       const sorted = [...(data || [])].sort((a: any, b: any) => String(a.code || '').localeCompare(String(b.code || ''), undefined, { numeric: true }));
8974:       setList(sorted);
8975:     } catch {}
8976:     finally { setLoading(false); }
8977:   };
8978:   useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);
8979: 
8980:   const filtered = q ? list.filter((p: any) => (String(p.code || '') + ' ' + String(p.name || '')).toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())) : list;
8981: 
8982:   const getNextPartnerCode = (listData: any[], draftData: any[], excludeIdx: number = -1) => {
8983:     const codes = [...listData.map(x => String(x.code || '')), ...draftData.filter((_, i) => i !== excludeIdx).map(x => String(x.code || ''))].filter(Boolean);
8984:     if (!codes.length) return '';
8985:     let best: any = null;
8986:     codes.forEach(c => {
8987:       const m = c.match(/^(.*?)(\d+)$/);
8988:       if (m) { const n = parseInt(m[2], 10); if (!best || n > best.n) best = { prefix: m[1], n, width: m[2].length }; }
8989:     });
8990:     if (best) return best.prefix + String(best.n + 1).padStart(best.width, '0');
8991:     return codes[codes.length - 1] + '-2';
8992:   };
8993: 
8994:   const autoPartnerCode = () => {
8995:     if (!editing || editing.id || editing.local_id) return;
8996:     if (String(editing.code || '').trim()) return;
8997:     const next = getNextPartnerCode(list, [], -1);
8998:     if (next) setEditing({ ...editing, code: next });
8999:   };
9000: 
9001:   const save = async () => {
9002:     if (!editing) return;
9003:     if (!editing.name) { showToast('نام الزامی است', true); return; }
9004:     const dupName = list.find((x: any) => (x.local_id !== editing.local_id) && (x.id !== editing.id) && String(x.name || '').trim() === String(editing.name || '').trim());
9005:     if (dupName) { showToast('⚠️ این نام قبلاً ثبت شده', true); return; }
9006:     let finalCode = editing.code;
9007:     if (!finalCode) finalCode = getNextPartnerCode(list, [], -1);
9008:     try {
9009:       const payload: any = { ...editing, code: finalCode };
9010:       delete payload.id;
9011:       if (editing.id || editing.local_id) await updatePartner(editing.local_id || editing.id, payload);
9012:       else await createPartner(payload);
9013:       showToast('✅ شریک ذخیره شد');
9014:       setEditing(null);
9015:       load();
9016:     } catch (e: any) { showToast(e?.message || String(e), true); }
9017:   };
9018: 
9019:   const remove = async (p: any) => {
9020:     const ok = await confirmMsg('حذف شریک', '«' + (p.name || '') + '» حذف شود؟');
9021:     if (!ok) return;
9022:     try { await deletePartner(p.local_id || p.id); load(); showToast('✅ حذف شد'); }
9023:     catch (e: any) { showToast(e?.message || String(e), true); }
9024:   };
9025: 
9026:   const startNew = () => {
9027:     const nextCode = getNextPartnerCode(list, drafts);
9028:     setDrafts((prev: any[]) => [...prev, { local_id: '', code: nextCode, name: '', phone: '', share_percent: 0, status: 'active', note: '' }]);
9029:   };
9030:   const updateDraft = (idx: number, field: string, value: any) => setDrafts(prev => prev.map((d, i) => i === idx ? { ...d, [field]: value } : d));
9031:   const removeDraft = (idx: number) => setDrafts(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx));
9032: 
9033:   const saveDrafts = async () => {
9034:     if (!drafts.length) return;
9035:     for (const d of drafts) if (!d.name) { showToast('نام در همه ردیف‌ها الزامی است', true); return; }
9036:     for (let i = 0; i < drafts.length; i++) {
9037:       const name = String(drafts[i].name || '').trim();
9038:       if (list.find((x: any) => String(x.name || '').trim() === name)) { showToast('⚠️ نام «' + name + '» قبلاً ثبت شده', true); return; }
9039:       if (drafts.find((x: any, j: number) => j !== i && String(x.name || '').trim() === name)) { showToast('⚠️ نام «' + name + '» در ردیف‌های دیگر تکراری است', true); return; }
9040:     }
9041:     const newDrafts = [...drafts];
9042:     for (let i = 0; i < newDrafts.length; i++) if (!newDrafts[i].code) newDrafts[i].code = getNextPartnerCode(list, newDrafts, i);
9043:     let success = 0;
9044:     for (const d of newDrafts) {
9045:       try { await createPartner(d); success++; } catch (e: any) { showToast('خطا: ' + (e?.message || String(e)), true); }
9046:     }
9047:     if (success) { showToast('✅ ' + success + ' شریک ذخیره شد'); setDrafts([]); load(); }
9048:   };
9049: 
9050:   const loadBanks = async (partner: any) => {
9051:     setBankLoading(true);
9052:     try { const data = await getPartnerBankAccounts(partner.code); setBankList(data || []); } catch {}
9053:     finally { setBankLoading(false); }
9054:   };
9055:   const openBanks = async (partner: any) => { setBankPartner(partner); setBankDrafts([]); await loadBanks(partner); };
9056:   const addBankDraft = () => setBankDrafts(prev => [...prev, { local_id: '', bank_name: '', currency: 'AFN', account_number: '', card_number: '', iban: '', holder_name: bankPartner?.name || '', is_default: false, status: 'active', note: '' }]);
9057:   const updateBankDraft = (idx: number, field: string, value: any) => setBankDrafts(prev => prev.map((d, i) => i === idx ? { ...d, [field]: value } : d));
9058:   const removeBankDraft = (idx: number) => setBankDrafts(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx));
9059:   const saveBankDrafts = async () => {
9060:     if (!bankPartner || !bankDrafts.length) return;
9061:     let success = 0;
9062:     for (const d of bankDrafts) {
9063:       try { await createPartnerBankAccount({ ...d, partner_code: bankPartner.code, partner_name: bankPartner.name }); success++; } catch (e: any) { showToast('خطا: ' + (e?.message || String(e)), true); }
9064:     }
9065:     if (success) { showToast('✅ ' + success + ' کارت ذخیره شد'); setBankDrafts([]); await loadBanks(bankPartner); }
9066:   };
9067:   const removeBank = async (b: any) => {
9068:     const ok = await confirmMsg('حذف کارت', 'حذف شود؟');
9069:     if (!ok) return;
9070:     try { await deletePartnerBankAccount(b.local_id || b.id, bankPartner.code); await loadBanks(bankPartner); showToast('✅ حذف شد'); } catch (e: any) { showToast(e?.message || String(e), true); }
9071:   };
9072: 
9073:   const openTransactions = async (partner: any) => {
9074:     setTxnPartner(partner);
9075:     setTxnLoading(true);
9076:     try {
9077:       const txns = await getPartnerTransactions(partner.code);
9078:       setTxnList(txns || []);
9079:       const banks = await getPartnerBankAccounts(partner.code);
9080:       setTxnBanks(banks || []);
9081:       setTxnForm({ type: 'deposit', currency: 'AFN', amount: '', bank_account_local_id: banks?.[0]?.local_id || '', box_effect: 'none', box_id: '', box_currency: '', exchange_rate: 1, to_partner_code: '', to_currency: 'AFN', to_amount: '', transaction_date: new Date().toISOString(), note: '', account_type: 'current', counterpart_type: 'none', counterpart_code: '' });
9082:     } catch {}
9083:     finally { setTxnLoading(false); }
9084:   };
9085: 
9086:   const reloadTransactions = async () => {
9087:     if (!txnPartner) return;
9088:     const txns = await getPartnerTransactions(txnPartner.code);
9089:     setTxnList(txns || []);
9090:   };
9091: 
9092:   const submitTxn = async () => {
9093:     if (!txnPartner) return;
9094:     const amt = parseFloat(String(txnForm.amount).replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '')) || 0;
9095:     if (!amt || amt <= 0) { showToast('مبلغ معتبر وارد کن', true); return; }
9096:     const type = txnForm.type;
9097:     const base: any = {
9098:       partner_code: txnPartner.code,
9099:       partner_name: txnPartner.name,
9100:       currency: txnForm.currency || 'AFN',
9101:       amount: amt,
9102:       bank_account_local_id: txnForm.bank_account_local_id || '',
9103:       box_effect: txnForm.box_effect || 'none',
9104:       box_id: txnForm.box_id || '',
9105:       box_currency: txnForm.box_currency || txnForm.currency,
9106:       exchange_rate: parseFloat(String(txnForm.exchange_rate)) || 1,
9107:       transaction_date: txnForm.transaction_date || new Date().toISOString(),
9108:       note: txnForm.note || '',
9109:       account_type: txnForm.account_type || 'current',
9110:       counterpart_type: txnForm.counterpart_type || 'none',
9111:       counterpart_code: txnForm.counterpart_code || '',
9112:     };
9113:     try {
9114:       if (type === 'transfer_out') {
9115:         if (!txnForm.to_partner_code) { showToast('شریک مقصد را انتخاب کن', true); return; }
9116:         const toPartner = list.find((x: any) => x.code === txnForm.to_partner_code);
9117:         if (!toPartner) { showToast('شریک مقصد پیدا نشد', true); return; }
9118:         const toAmt = parseFloat(String(txnForm.to_amount).replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '')) || amt;
9119:         const group = 'tr_' + Date.now();
9120:         await createPartnerTransaction({ ...base, type: 'transfer_out', to_partner_code: toPartner.code, to_currency: txnForm.to_currency || base.currency, to_amount: toAmt, transfer_group: group, box_effect: 'none' });
9121:         await createPartnerTransaction({ ...base, partner_code: toPartner.code, partner_name: toPartner.name, type: 'transfer_in', currency: txnForm.to_currency || base.currency, amount: toAmt, to_partner_code: txnPartner.code, to_currency: base.currency, to_amount: amt, transfer_group: group, box_effect: 'none' });
9122:         showToast('✅ انتقال ثبت شد');
9123:       } else {
9124:         await createPartnerTransaction({ ...base, type });
9125:         showToast(type === 'deposit' ? '✅ واریز ثبت شد' : '✅ برداشت ثبت شد');
9126:       }
9127:       setTxnForm({ ...txnForm, amount: '', to_amount: '', note: '' });
9128:       await reloadTransactions();
9129:       load();
9130:     } catch (e: any) { showToast('خطا: ' + (e?.message || String(e)), true); }
9131:   };
9132: 
9133:   const removeTxn = async (t: any) => {
9134:     const ok = await confirmMsg('حذف حرکت', 'حذف شود؟');
9135:     if (!ok) return;
9136:     try { await deletePartnerTransaction(t.local_id || t.id, txnPartner.code); await reloadTransactions(); showToast('✅ حذف شد'); }
9137:     catch (e: any) { showToast(e?.message || String(e), true); }
9138:   };
9139: 
9140:   const currencies = ['AFN', 'USD', 'EUR', 'PKR', 'IRR'];
9141: 
9142:   return (
9143:     <View>
9144:       <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 6, marginBottom: 10 }}>
9145:         <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#7c3aed' }]} onPress={startNew}>
9146:           <Text style={s.btnTxt}>➕ افزودن ردیف</Text>
9147:         </TouchableOpacity>
9148:         {drafts.length > 0 && (
9149:           <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { backgroundColor: '#059669' }]} onPress={saveDrafts}>
9150:             <Text style={s.btnTxt}>💾 ذخیره همه ({drafts.length})</Text>
9151:           </TouchableOpacity>
9152:         )}

9380:                   <TextInput style={[s.inp, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={txnForm.note} onChangeText={(v) => setTxnForm({ ...txnForm, note: v })} placeholder="اختیاری" />
9381:                 </View>

'#dc2626' : '#7c3aed', fontWeight: 'bold' }}>{t.type === 'deposit' ? 'واریز' : t.type === 'withdraw' ? 'برداشت' : t.type === 'transfer_out' ? 'انتقال' : 'دریافت'}</Text>
9408:                       <Text style={{ width: 70, fontSize: 9, textAlign: 'center', color: '#7c3aed' }}>{t.account_type === 'capital' ? 'سرمایه' : t.account_type === 'drawings' ? 'برداشت' : 'جاری'}</Text>

'بانک' : t.counterpart_type === 'customer' ? 'مشتری' : t.counterpart_type === 'supplier' ? 'تأمین‌کننده' : t.counterpart_type === 'partner' ? 'شریک' : '—'}</Text>

9416:                     </View>
9417:                   ))}

9421:           </View>

9428: function BanksSection({ showToast }: any) {
9429:   const [list, setList] = useState<any[]>([]);
9430:   const [q, setQ] = useState('');
9431:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
9432:   const [editing, setEditing] = useState<any>(null);
9433:   const [drafts, setDrafts] = useState<any[]>([]);
9434: 
9435:   const load = async () => {
9436:     setLoading(true);
9437:     try { setList(await getBanks()); } catch {}
9438:     finally { setLoading(false); }
9439:   };
9440:   useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);
9441: 
9442:   const filtered = q ? list.filter((p: any) => (String(p.name || '') + ' ' + String(p.code || '')).toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())) : list;
9443: 
9444:   const save = async () => {
9445:     if (!editing) return;
9446:     if (!editing.name) { showToast('نام بانک الزامی است', true); return; }
9447:     const dup = list.find((x: any) => (x.local_id !== editing.local_id) && (x.id !== editing.id) && String(x.name || '').trim() === String(editing.name || '').trim());
9448:     if (dup) { showToast('⚠️ این نام بانک قبلاً ثبت شده', true); return; }
9449:     try {
9450:       const payload = { ...editing }; delete payload.id;
9451:       if (editing.id || editing.local_id) await updateBank(editing.local_id || editing.id, payload);
9452:       else await createBank(payload);
9453:       showToast('✅ بانک ذخیره شد'); setEditing(null); load();
9454:     } catch (e: any) { showToast(e?.message || String(e), true); }
9455:   };
9456: 
9457:   const remove = async (p: any) => {
9458:     const ok = await confirmMsg('حذف بانک', '«' + (p.name || '') + '» حذف شود؟');
9459:     if (!ok) return;
9460:     try { await deleteBank(p.local_id || p.id); load(); showToast('✅ حذف شد'); }
9461:     catch (e: any) { showToast(e?.message || String(e), true); }
9462:   };
9463: 
9464:   const startNew = () => setDrafts(prev => [...prev, { local_id: '', name: '', code: '', note: '' }]);
9465:   const updateDraft = (idx: number, field: string, value: any) => setDrafts(prev => prev.map((d, i) => i === idx ? { ...d, [field]: value } : d));
9466:   const removeDraft = (idx: number) => setDrafts(prev => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx));
9467: 
9468:   const saveDrafts = async () => {
9469:     if (!drafts.length) return;
9470:     for (const d of drafts) if (!d.name) { showToast('نام در همه ردیف‌ها الزامی است', true); return; }
9471:     for (let i = 0; i < drafts.length; i++) {
9472:       const name = String(drafts[i].name || '').trim();
9473:       if (list.find((x: any) => String(x.name || '').trim() === name)) { showToast('⚠️ نام «' + name + '» قبلاً ثبت شده', true); return; }
9474:       if (drafts.find((x: any, j: number) => j !== i && String(x.name || '').trim() === name)) { showToast('⚠️ نام «' + name + '» در ردیف‌ها تکراری است', true); return; }
9475:     }
9476:     let success = 0;
9477:     for (const d of drafts) { try { await createBank(d); success++; } catch (e: any) { showToast(e?.message || String(e), true); } }
9478:     if (success) { showToast('✅ ' + success + ' بانک ذخیره شد'); setDrafts([]); load(); }
9479:   };
9480: 
9481:   return (
9482:     <View>
9483:       <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 6, marginBottom: 10 }}>
9484:         <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#7c3aed' }]} onPress={startNew}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>➕ افزودن بانک</Text></TouchableOpacity>
9485:         {drafts.length > 0 && <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { backgroundColor: '#059669' }]} onPress={saveDrafts}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>💾 ذخیره ({drafts.length})</Text></TouchableOpacity>}
9486:       </View>
9487:       <TextInput style={[s.inp, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={q} onChangeText={setQ} placeholder="🔍 جستجو در بانک‌ها..." placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
9488:       <Text style={s.secT}>🏦 بانک‌ها ({toFaNum(filtered.length)})</Text>
9489:       <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator>
9490:         <View>
9491:           <View style={[s.tblHeader, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse', position: 'relative', zIndex: 10 }]}>
9492:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 40 }]}>#</Text>
9493:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 250 }]}>نام بانک</Text>
9494:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 120 }]}>کد</Text>
9495:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 200 }]}>یادداشت</Text>
9496:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 100 }]}>عملیات</Text>
9497:           </View>
9498:           <ScrollView style={{ maxHeight: 420 }} nestedScrollEnabled>
9499:             {drafts.map((d, idx) => (
9500:               <View key={'bd-' + idx} style={[s.tblRow, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse', backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }]}>
9501:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 40, textAlign: 'center', color: '#d4af37', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>D{idx + 1}</Text>
9502:                 <View style={{ width: 250, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}><TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(d.name || '')} onChangeText={(v) => updateDraft(idx, 'name', v)} placeholder="نام بانک *" /></View>
9503:                 <View style={{ width: 120, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}><TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(d.code || '')} onChangeText={(v) => updateDraft(idx, 'code', v)} placeholder="کد" /></View>
9504:                 <View style={{ width: 200, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}><TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(d.note || '')} onChangeText={(v) => updateDraft(idx, 'note', v)} placeholder="یادداشت" /></View>
9505:                 <View style={{ width: 100, alignItems: 'center' }}><TouchableOpacity onPress={() => removeDraft(idx)} style={{ backgroundColor: '#fee2e2', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text>✕</Text></TouchableOpacity></View>
9506:               </View>
9507:             ))}
9508:             {editing && (
9509:               <View style={[s.tblRow, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse', backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }]}>
9510:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 40 }]}>✏️</Text>
9511:                 <View style={{ width: 250, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}><TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(editing.name || '')} onChangeText={(v) => setEditing({ ...editing, name: v })} placeholder="نام بانک *" /></View>
9512:                 <View style={{ width: 120, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}><TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(editing.code || '')} onChangeText={(v) => setEditing({ ...editing, code: v })} placeholder="کد" /></View>
9513:                 <View style={{ width: 200, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}><TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(editing.note || '')} onChangeText={(v) => setEditing({ ...editing, note: v })} placeholder="یادداشت" /></View>
9514:                 <View style={{ width: 100, flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 4, justifyContent: 'center' }}>
9515:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={save} style={{ backgroundColor: '#059669', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text>💾</Text></TouchableOpacity>
9516:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setEditing(null)} style={{ backgroundColor: '#64748b', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text>✕</Text></TouchableOpacity>
9517:                 </View>
9518:               </View>
9519:             )}
9520:             {loading ? <ActivityIndicator color="#d4af37" style={{ marginTop: 20 }} /> : filtered.map((p: any, i: number) => (
9521:               <View key={p.local_id || p.id || i} style={[s.tblRow, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse' }, i % 2 === 0 && { backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }]}>
9522:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 40, fontSize: 12, color: '#d4af37', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{i + 1}</Text>
9523:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 250, fontSize: 12, color: C.text, fontWeight: 'bold', textAlign: 'right' }]} numberOfLines={1}>{p.name || '—'}</Text>
9524:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 120, fontSize: 11, color: '#7c3aed' }]}>{p.code || '—'}</Text>
9525:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 200, fontSize: 10, color: C.textMut, textAlign: 'right' }]} numberOfLines={1}>{p.note || '—'}</Text>
9526:                 <View style={[s.tdCell, { width: 100, flexDirection: 'row', gap: 4, justifyContent: 'center' }]}>
9527:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setEditing({ ...p })} style={{ backgroundColor: '#dbeafe', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text>✏️</Text></TouchableOpacity>
9528:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => remove(p)} style={{ backgroundColor: '#fee2e2', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text>🗑</Text></TouchableOpacity>
9529:                 </View>
9530:               </View>
9531:             ))}
9532:           </ScrollView>
9533:         </View>
9534:       </ScrollView>
9535:     </View>
9536:   );
9537: }

9632:               <View key={p.local_id || p.id || i} style={[s.tblRow, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse' }, i % 2 === 0 && { backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }]}>
9633:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 40, fontSize: 12, color: '#d4af37', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{i + 1}</Text>
9634:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 250, fontSize: 12, color: C.text, fontWeight: 'bold', textAlign: 'right' }]} numberOfLines={1}>{p.name || '—'}</Text>
9635:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 150, fontSize: 11, color: C.text }]}>{p.phone || '—'}</Text>
9636:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 200, fontSize: 10, color: C.textMut, textAlign: 'right' }]} numberOfLines={1}>{p.note || '—'}</Text>
9637:                 <View style={[s.tdCell, { width: 100, flexDirection: 'row', gap: 4, justifyContent: 'center' }]}>
9638:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setEditing({ ...p })} style={{ backgroundColor: '#dbeafe', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text>✏️</Text></TouchableOpacity>
9639:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => remove(p)} style={{ backgroundColor: '#fee2e2', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text>🗑</Text></TouchableOpacity>
9640:                 </View>
9641:               </View>
9642:             ))}
9643:           </ScrollView>
9644:         </View>
9645:       </ScrollView>
9646:     </View>

9650: function SourcesSection({ showToast }: any) {
9651:   const [sub, setSub] = useState<'parties' | 'products' | 'partners' | 'banks' | 'shippings'>('parties');
9652:   return (

9657:           <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSub('products')} style={[s.btn, { backgroundColor: sub === 'products' ? '#7c3aed' : '#334155', paddingHorizontal: 12 }]}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>📦 کالاها</Text></TouchableOpacity>
9658:           <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSub('partners')} style={[s.btn, { backgroundColor: sub === 'partners' ? '#7c3aed' : '#334155', paddingHorizontal: 12 }]}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>👥 شرکا</Text></TouchableOpacity>
9659:           <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSub('banks')} style={[s.btn, { backgroundColor: sub === 'banks' ? '#7c3aed' : '#334155', paddingHorizontal: 12 }]}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>🏦 بانک‌ها</Text></TouchableOpacity>
9660:           <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setSub('shippings')} style={[s.btn, { backgroundColor: sub === 'shippings' ? '#7c3aed' : '#334155', paddingHorizontal: 12 }]}><Text style={s.btnTxt}>🚚 باربری‌ها</Text></TouchableOpacity>
9661:         </View>
9662:       </ScrollView>
9663:       {sub === 'parties' ? <PartiesSection showToast={showToast} /> : sub === 'products' ? <ProductsSection showToast={showToast} /> : sub === 'partners' ? <PartnersSection showToast={showToast} /> : sub === 'banks' ? <BanksSection showToast={showToast} /> : <ShippingsSection showToast={showToast} />}
9664:     </View>

9668: function ProductsSection({ showToast }: any) {
9669:   const [list, setList] = useState<any[]>([]);
9670:   const [q, setQ] = useState('');
9671:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
9672:   const [editing, setEditing] = useState<any>(null);
9673:   const [drafts, setDrafts] = useState<any[]>([]);
9674: 
9675:   const load = async () => {
9676:     setLoading(true);
9677:     try {
9678:       const data = await getProducts();
9679:       const sorted = [...(data || [])].sort((a: any, b: any) => {
9680:         const ca = String(a.code || '');
9681:         const cb = String(b.code || '');
9682:         if (!ca && !cb) return 0;
9683:         if (!ca) return 1;
9684:         if (!cb) return -1;
9685:         return ca.localeCompare(cb, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
9686:       });
9687:       setList(sorted);
9688:     } catch {}
9689:     finally { setLoading(false); }
9690:   };
9691:   useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);
9692: 
9693:   const filtered = q
9694:     ? list.filter((p: any) => (String(p.code || '') + ' ' + String(p.name || '') + ' ' + String(p.shelf || '')).toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase()))
9695:     : list;
9696: 
9697:   const getNextProductCode = (listData: any[], draftData: any[], excludeIdx: number = -1) => {
9698:     const codes = [
9699:       ...listData.map(x => String(x.code || '')),
9700:       ...draftData.filter((_, i) => i !== excludeIdx).map(x => String(x.code || ''))
9701:     ].filter(Boolean);
9702:     if (!codes.length) return '';
9703:     let best: any = null;
9704:     codes.forEach(c => {
9705:       const m = c.match(/^(.*?)(\d+)$/);
9706:       if (m) {
9707:         const n = parseInt(m[2], 10);
9708:         if (!best || n > best.n) best = { prefix: m[1], n, width: m[2].length };
9709:       }
9710:     });
9711:     if (best) return best.prefix + String(best.n + 1).padStart(best.width, '0');
9712:     return codes[codes.length - 1] + '-2';
9713:   };
9714: 
9715:   const autoProductCode = () => {
9716:     if (!editing || editing.id || editing.local_id) return;
9717:     if (String(editing.code || '').trim()) return;
9718:     const next = getNextProductCode(list, [], -1);
9719:     if (next) setEditing({ ...editing, code: next });
9720:   };
9721: 
9722:   const checkDuplicateName = (name: string, excludeLocalId?: string, excludeId?: string) => {
9723:     const n = String(name || '').trim();
9724:     if (!n) return null;
9725:     return list.find((x: any) => (x.local_id !== excludeLocalId) && (x.id !== excludeId) && String(x.name || '').trim() === n) || null;
9726:   };
9727: 
9728:   const save = async () => {
9729:     if (!editing) return;
9730:     if (!editing.name) { showToast('نام الزامی است', true); return; }
9731:     const dupName = checkDuplicateName(editing.name, editing.local_id, editing.id);
9732:     if (dupName) { showToast('⚠️ این نام کالا قبلاً ثبت شده', true); return; }
9733:     const dupCode = list.find((x: any) => (x.local_id !== editing.local_id) && (x.id !== editing.id) && String(x.code || '').trim() === String(editing.code || '').trim());
9734:     if (dupCode && editing.code) { showToast('⚠️ این کد کالا قبلاً ثبت شده', true); return; }
9735:     let finalCode = editing.code;
9736:     if (!finalCode) finalCode = getNextProductCode(list, [], -1);
9737:     try {
9738:       const payload: any = { ...editing, code: finalCode };
9739:       delete payload.id;
9740:       if (editing.id || editing.local_id) await updateProduct(editing.local_id || editing.id, payload);
9741:       else await createProduct(payload);
9742:       showToast('✅ کالا ذخیره شد');
9743:       setEditing(null);
9744:       load();
9745:     } catch (e: any) { showToast(e?.message || String(e), true); }
9746:   };
9747: 
9748:   const remove = async (p: any) => {
9749:     const ok = await confirmMsg('حذف کالا', '«' + (p.name || '') + '» حذف شود؟');
9750:     if (!ok) return;
9751:     try { await deleteProduct(p.local_id || p.id); load(); showToast('✅ حذف شد'); }
9752:     catch (e: any) { showToast(e?.message || String(e), true); }
9753:   };
9754: 
9755:   const startNew = () => {
9756:     const nextCode = getNextProductCode(list, drafts);
9757:     setDrafts((prev: any[]) => [...prev, { id: null, code: nextCode, name: '', price: 0, shelf: '' }]);
9758:   };
9759: 
9760:   const updateDraft = (idx: number, field: string, value: any) => {
9761:     setDrafts((prev: any[]) => prev.map((d, i) => i === idx ? { ...d, [field]: value } : d));
9762:   };
9763: 
9764:   const removeDraft = (idx: number) => {
9765:     setDrafts((prev: any[]) => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx));
9766:   };
9767: 
9768:   const saveDrafts = async () => {
9769:     if (!drafts.length) return;
9770:     for (const d of drafts) if (!d.name) { showToast('نام در همه ردیف‌ها الزامی است', true); return; }
9771: 
9772:     // چک نام تکراری
9773:     for (let i = 0; i < drafts.length; i++) {
9774:       const name = String(drafts[i].name || '').trim();
9775:       const existing = list.find((x: any) => String(x.name || '').trim() === name);
9776:       if (existing) {
9777:         showToast('⚠️ نام «' + name + '» قبلاً ثبت شده است', true);
9778:         return;
9779:       }
9780:       const otherDraft = drafts.find((x: any, j: number) => j !== i && String(x.name || '').trim() === name);
9781:       if (otherDraft) {
9782:         showToast('⚠️ نام «' + name + '» در ردیف‌های دیگر تکراری است', true);
9783:         return;
9784:       }
9785:     }
9786: 
9787:     const newDrafts = [...drafts];
9788:     for (let i = 0; i < newDrafts.length; i++) {
9789:       let d = newDrafts[i];
9790:       if (!d.code) d.code = getNextProductCode(list, newDrafts, i);
9791:     }
9792: 
9793:     let success = 0;
9794:     for (const d of newDrafts) {
9795:       try { await createProduct(d); success++; }
9796:       catch (e: any) { showToast('خطا: ' + (e?.message || String(e)), true); }
9797:     }
9798:     if (success) {
9799:       showToast('✅ ' + success + ' کالا ذخیره شد');
9800:       setDrafts([]);
9801:       load();
9802:     }
9803:   };
9804: 
9805:   return (
9806:     <View>
9807:       <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 6, marginBottom: 10 }}>
9808:         <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#7c3aed' }]} onPress={startNew}>
9809:           <Text style={s.btnTxt}>➕ افزودن ردیف</Text>
9810:         </TouchableOpacity>
9811:         {drafts.length > 0 && (
9812:           <TouchableOpacity style={[s.btn, { backgroundColor: '#059669' }]} onPress={saveDrafts}>
9813:             <Text style={s.btnTxt}>💾 ذخیره همه ({drafts.length})</Text>
9814:           </TouchableOpacity>
9815:         )}
9816:       </View>
9817: 
9818:       <TextInput style={[s.inp, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={q} onChangeText={setQ} placeholder="🔍 جستجو در کالاها..." placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
9819:       <Text style={s.secT}>📦 کالاها ({toFaNum(filtered.length)})</Text>
9820: 
9821:       <ScrollView horizontal showsHorizontalScrollIndicator>
9822:         <View>
9823:           <View style={[s.tblHeader, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse', position: 'relative', zIndex: 10 }]}>
9824:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 40 }]}>#</Text>
9825:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 200 }]}>نام</Text>
9826:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 130 }]}>قیمت</Text>
9827:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 120 }]}>قفسه</Text>
9828:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 140 }]}>کد</Text>
9829:             <Text style={[s.thCell, { width: 100 }]}>عملیات</Text>
9830:           </View>
9831: 
9832:           <ScrollView style={{ maxHeight: 420 }} nestedScrollEnabled>
9833:             {drafts.map((d, idx) => (
9834:               <View key={'draft-' + idx} style={[s.tblRow, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse', backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }]}>
9835:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 40, textAlign: 'center', color: '#d4af37', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>D{idx + 1}</Text>
9836:                 <View style={{ width: 200, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
9837:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(d.name || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => updateDraft(idx, 'name', v)} placeholder="نام *" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
9838:                 </View>
9839:                 <View style={{ width: 130, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
9840:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(d.price || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => updateDraft(idx, 'price', parseFloat(v) || 0)} keyboardType="numeric" placeholder="قیمت" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
9841:                 </View>
9842:                 <View style={{ width: 120, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
9843:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(d.shelf || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => updateDraft(idx, 'shelf', v)} placeholder="قفسه" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
9844:                 </View>
9845:                 <View style={{ width: 140, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
9846:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(d.code || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => updateDraft(idx, 'code', v)} placeholder="کد" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
9847:                 </View>
9848:                 <View style={{ width: 100, flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 4, justifyContent: 'center' }}>
9849:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => removeDraft(idx)} style={{ backgroundColor: '#fee2e2', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text style={{ color: '#fff', fontSize: 12 }}>✕</Text></TouchableOpacity>
9850:                 </View>
9851:               </View>
9852:             ))}
9853: 
9854:             {editing && (
9855:               <View style={[s.tblRow, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse', backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }]}>
9856:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 40 }]}>✏️</Text>
9857:                 <View style={{ width: 200, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
9858:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(editing.name || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => setEditing({ ...editing, name: v })} onEndEditing={autoProductCode} placeholder="نام *" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
9859:                 </View>
9860:                 <View style={{ width: 130, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
9861:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(editing.price || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => setEditing({ ...editing, price: parseFloat(v) || 0 })} keyboardType="numeric" placeholder="قیمت" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
9862:                 </View>
9863:                 <View style={{ width: 120, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
9864:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(editing.shelf || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => setEditing({ ...editing, shelf: v })} placeholder="قفسه" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
9865:                 </View>
9866:                 <View style={{ width: 140, paddingHorizontal: 2 }}>
9867:                   <TextInput style={[s.tdInput, { backgroundColor: C.input, color: C.text, borderColor: C.border }]} value={String(editing.code || '')} onChangeText={(v: string) => setEditing({ ...editing, code: v })} placeholder="کد" placeholderTextColor={C.textMut} />
9868:                 </View>
9869:                 <View style={{ width: 100, flexDirection: 'row-reverse', gap: 4, justifyContent: 'center' }}>
9870:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={save} style={{ backgroundColor: '#059669', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text style={{ color: '#fff', fontSize: 12 }}>💾</Text></TouchableOpacity>
9871:                   <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setEditing(null)} style={{ backgroundColor: '#64748b', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6 }}><Text style={{ color: '#fff', fontSize: 12 }}>✕</Text></TouchableOpacity>
9872:                 </View>

9875:

9877:               <View key={p.local_id || p.id || i} style={[s.tblRow, { flexDirection: 'row-reverse' }, i % 2 === 0 && { backgroundColor: C.cardAlt }]}>
9878:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 40, fontSize: 12, color: '#d4af37', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{i + 1}</Text>
9879:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 200, fontSize: 12, color: C.text, fontWeight: 'bold', textAlign: 'right' }]} numberOfLines={1}>{p.name || '—'}</Text>
9880:                 <Text style={[s.tdCell, { width: 130, fontSize: 12, color: '#00ff88', fontWeight: 'bold' }]}>{fmt(p.price || 0)}</Text>

4353:     <ScrollView style={[s.content, { backgroundColor: C.bg }]} contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 12, paddingBottom: 60 }}>
4354: 
4355:       <TouchableOpacity style={[s.addBtn, { backgroundColor: '#166534', marginTop: 8 }]} onPress={() => { setView('form'); if (!items.length) addItem(); }}>
4356: 
4357:         <Text style={s.btnTxt}>{t('➕ فاکتور خرید جدید')}</Text>
4358: 
4359:       </TouchableOpacity>
4360: 
4361:       <EL text="📄 خریدها ({toFaNum(list.length)})" style={s.secT} />
4362: 
4363:             {loading ? <ActivityIndicator color="#34d399" /> : list.filter((inv: any) => !hiddenPurchases.includes(inv.invoice)).map((inv: any) => (
4364: 
4365:               <View key={inv.invoice} style={[s.invCard, { backgroundColor: C.card, borderRightColor: '#166534' }]}>
4366: 
4367:           <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row-reverse', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: 6 }}>
4368: 
4369:             <Text style={s.invNum}>{inv.invoice}</Text>
4370: 
4371:             <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => remove(inv.invoice)}>
4372: 
4373:               <Text style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>👁️</Text>
4374: 
4375:             </TouchableOpacity>
4376: 
4377:           </View>
4378: 
4379:           <Text style={s.invCust}>🏭 {inv.name}</Text>
4380: 
4381:           <Text style={s.invStat}>💰 {fmt(inv.total)} | 💳 {fmt(inv.paid)}</Text>
4382: 
4383:         </View>
4384: 
4385:       ))}
4386: 
4387:     </ScrollView>
4388: 
4389:   );
4390: 
4391: }
